Surface — the component paints no fill and no frame of its own, so it works on white and on --muted alike; the box around it is the page's (Card in the create flow's preview column, form-section --outlined on a detail page). Both instances above sit in their real container so the two surfaces are visible side by side.

Surface——本支不畫底色也不畫外框，白底與 --muted 灰底皆可；外面那個盒子歸頁面（建立頁預覽欄的 Card、細節頁的 form-section --outlined）。上面兩個實例各自坐在真正的容器裡，兩種表面可以並排對照。

For developers — classes · code給工程師 — class · 程式碼
.lctlThe three-row container. It holds the vertical rhythm in its own gap (--sp-24) — never .lctl__row + .lctl__row, per STYLE-DECISIONS Q103. It also zeroes .field's own bottom margin so the two do not stack into a double gap.三列的容器。垂直節奏放在自己的 gap（--sp-24），不用 .lctl__row + .lctl__row（STYLE-DECISIONS Q103）。同時把列內 .field 的下邊距歸零，免得與 gap 疊成兩段距離。
.lctl__row · --choiceOne switch = one row. --choice is for the create flow's listing row, where the control is a full-width radio list under the title instead of a switch at the end of the line.一個開關一列。--choice 給建立頁的上架列用：控制是整列寬的 radio-list、擺在標題下方，不是行尾的開關。
.lctl__head · __titles · __title · __hintTitle and its one line of explanation on the left, the row's control on the right. __hint is deliberately identical to .field__hint in size and colour — it is the same role ("what else do I need to know about this row"), so it must not become a second style for supporting text.左邊是標題與一行說明，右邊是這一列的控制。__hint 刻意與 .field__hint 同字級同色——那是同一個角色（這一列還要注意什麼），不該變成第二種輔助說明樣式。
.lctl__controlThe end-of-line control slot (a Switch). Does not shrink, and lines up with the first line of the title.行尾控制槽（放 Switch）。不被壓縮，並與標題第一行對齊。
.lctl__bodyA full-width control under the head — the collapsible radio list, or the Link field that opens when "show in shop" is turned off. Carries its own [hidden].擺在 head 底下的整列寬控制——收合式 radio-list，或關掉「顯示於商店」時展開的 Link field。自帶 [hidden]。
.lctl__sched · __gridThe schedule fields. __sched carries its own [hidden] so a collapsed row takes no space; __grid puts two datetime fields side by side and drops to one column under 720px. Add .lctl--stack on the container where the column is narrow by design (the create flow's 264px preview rail) rather than guessing from the viewport.排程欄。__sched 自帶 [hidden]，收起時不佔位；__grid 讓兩個時間欄並排，720px 以下收成一欄。欄本來就窄的情境（建立流程 264px 的預覽欄）在容器掛 .lctl--stack 明說，不靠猜視窗寬度。
.lctl__ruleThe hairline between the main decision and the second layer (2026-09-04). It runs to the card's inner edge (negative inline margins cancel the parent's --card-pad) because it separates the two halves of the section, not the second layer's own box — a border-top on that box would stop at its rounded frame.主決定與第 2 層之間的髮絲線（2026-09-04）。用負左右外距抵銷母卡的 --card-pad、畫到卡的內緣——它分的是這個區塊的兩段，不是第 2 層那個盒子自己的邊；在盒子上加 border-top 只會停在它的圓角框裡。
.lctl__derivedThe second layer (2026-09-04 · D245): everything that only exists once the main decision is made — listing / unlist time, show in shop, sale period. Whether it has a frame is the consumer's call — add .control-group.control-group--plain for one (2026-09-04: the listing section's second layer has a frame, the sale section's does not). The class itself only owns two things: internal rhythm via gap, and taking the bottom margin back off .field. Markup: <div class="control-group control-group--plain lctl__derived">.第 2 層（2026-09-04 · D245）：主決定成立之後才存在的一組設定——上架與下架時間、顯示於商店、販售窗口。要不要有外框由消費頁決定（加 .control-group.control-group--plain 就有；2026-09-04：上架設定那層有框、開賣設定那層沒有），本類只管兩件事：內部節奏交給 gap、把 .field 的下邊距收回容器。markup：<div class="control-group control-group--plain lctl__derived">。
.lctl__noteOne quiet line for the whole block (currently the time-zone caveat). --faint-ink, so it does not compete with the per-row hints.整區共用的一行淡說明（目前是時區待確認）。吃 --faint-ink，不與各列的 hint 搶注意力。

Product rules it renders它畫的是哪些產品規則 — spec §7.14: unlist time / sale start / sale end are all optional and the form must not pre-fill any of them (a default value decides for the creator); the only validation left is "sale end must be after sale start" (ListingState.validateSaleWindow) — D239's "the sale period must sit inside the listing period" no longer applies to products; a hidden item's private link is generated by ListingState.privateLinkFor() and dies when the item is unlisted.——規格 §7.14：下架日期與時間、開賣日期與時間、停售日期與時間三個都是選填，表單不得預設任何一個（預設值等於替創作者決定）；剩下的校驗只有「停售要晚於開賣」（ListingState.validateSaleWindow）——D239 的「販售窗口必須落在可見性窗口內」對商品已廢除；隱藏時的非公開連結由 ListingState.privateLinkFor() 產生，下架時連同公開連結一起失效。

4.206 Link field

🟡 molecule

Purpose

用途

A link the system generated, a person cannot edit, and needs to be able to carry away whole. First role: the private link of a hidden product or bundle (spec §7.14) — the creator copies it to someone, or resets it to kill the old one.

一條系統產生、人不能編輯、但要能整條帶走的連結。首個角色是隱藏商品或組合的非公開連結（規格 §7.14）——創作者複製給人，或重置作廢舊的那條。

2026-10-15 — screen-reader feedback: js/link-field.js adds a visually hidden aria-live="polite" region inside each [data-linkf] and writes “Copied” into it after a successful copy, so screen readers announce it (the button’s own text swap is often not read). No markup change for consuming pages. New consumer: the payment ID on the order detail uses --stack, so the last character is no longer cut off.

2026-10-15 讀屏提示：js/link-field.js 在每個 [data-linkf] 裡補一塊看不見的 aria-live="polite" 區，複製成功時寫入「已複製」讓讀屏唸出來（按鈕換字常常不會被讀）。消費頁 markup 不用改。新消費情境：訂單詳情的付款編號改用 --stack，最後一個字不再被切掉。

The feedback lives on the button. "Copied" replaces the button's own label instead of raising a toast: the place your hand just left is the place your eyes are looking, so feedback that flies to a corner of the screen is no feedback. Vault share's .vshare__link is where the site first did this and it stays as it is for now — the vault's link also carries a "just generated" orange frame, which is a different thing from this component's neutral default. Do not copy that markup; when the two are confirmed to be one look, they converge then.

回饋長在按鈕上。「已複製」直接換掉按鈕自己的字，不跳 toast：手剛離開的地方就是眼睛在看的地方，回饋跑到畫面角落等於沒有回饋。Vault share 的 .vshare__link 是站上第一個做這件事的地方，這一輪維持原狀——媒體庫那條連結還多帶「剛產生」的橘框強調，與本支的中性預設不是同一件事。別照抄它的 markup；日後確認兩者要同一個外觀時再收斂。

2026-09-30 (D340): also the event link that appears when an event’s Display settings switch is off (Create event step 7, Event detail → Setup) — copy and reset, and switching the event back to shown revokes it.

2026-09-30（D340）：活動的「顯示設定」關掉（隱藏）時長出來的活動連結也用這一支（建立活動第 6 步、活動詳情 → 設定）——可複製、可重置，切回顯示＝撤銷。

Rendered preview (live — Copy really writes to the clipboard, driven by js/link-field.js)

視覺展示 （複製鈕是真的會寫進剪貼簿，由 js/link-field.js 驅動）

Default

預設

窄欄 （--stack，給約 250–300px 的窄欄——product-detail.html／bundle-detail.html 的右欄）

Private link

The old link stops working immediately

For developers — classes · API · code給工程師 — class · API · 程式碼
.linkf__rowThe frame: --input-surface fill, 1px --border, --radius-lg. The input inside goes transparent with a transparent border — the frame is already drawn once, and drawing it twice reads as a box inside a box.外框：--input-surface 底、1px --border、--radius-lg。裡面的輸入框轉透明、邊框也透明——框已經畫過一次，再畫一次會變成框中框。
.linkf__inputRead-only, monospace. A link gets read aloud and checked character by character, so it goes in --font-mono at --fs-12.唯讀、等寬。連結要被唸出來、也要被逐字核對，所以走 --font-mono ＋ --fs-12。
.linkf__act · --copyAction slot; looks come from Button. --copy adds a 92px minimum width so the row does not jump when "Copy" becomes "Copied".動作槽，外觀借 Button。--copy 給 92px 下限寬度，讓「複製」換成「已複製」時整列不跳動。
.linkf--copiedThe row lights up (--primary border + --selected-surface) alongside the button's label change, so the feedback is not only three characters wide.整列亮起（--primary 邊框＋--selected-surface），與按鈕換字同時發生，回饋不只有三個字那麼寬。
.linkf--disabledVoid link: --muted fill, --locked-field-ink text, pointer events off on the actions. Colour and cursor say it, never a stacked opacity (STYLE-DECISIONS Q58).連結已作廢：--muted 底、--locked-field-ink 文字、動作鈕不吃點擊。靠顏色與游標表達，不疊 opacity 弱化整塊（STYLE-DECISIONS Q58）。
.linkf--stackNarrow-column variant: the row wraps, the input takes the full first line (flex:1 1 100%), and the two action buttons fall to a second line right-aligned (justify-content:flex-end, the site's usual trailing-action-row rule). Used in product-detail.html／bundle-detail.html's right rail, where the column is only ~250–300px and the default single row squeezes the link down to "https".窄欄變體：整列改 wrap，輸入框獨佔第一行（flex:1 1 100%），兩顆動作鈕落到第二行靠右（justify-content:flex-end，站上動作列一貫的靠右規則）。用在 product-detail.html／bundle-detail.html 的右欄——那裡只有約 250–300px，預設單列會把連結擠到只剩「https」。

JS module — js/link-field.jsJS 模組 — js/link-field.js

Data-attribute contract: Data-attribute 契約：data-linkf on the root · 掛在根節點 · data-linkf-copy on the copy button · 掛在複製鈕 · data-linkf-reset on the reset button. Reset does not invent a new URL — it fires linkfield:reset and the page answers, normally with ListingState.resetPrivateLink(entity), then writes the value back with linkField.setValue(root, url).掛在重置鈕。重置不自己決定新連結長什麼樣——它發一顆 linkfield:reset 事件，由頁面回答（通常呼叫 ListingState.resetPrivateLink(entity)），再用 linkField.setValue(root, url) 寫回。

window.linkField.setValue(root, url) · value(root) · copy(root). Clicks run through one document-level delegate, so link rows rendered later need no rebinding. Clipboard writes fall back to an off-screen input + execCommand on non-secure origins.點擊走 document 級單一委派，之後才渲染出來的連結列不必重新綁定。非安全來源沒有 clipboard API 時退回離屏 input ＋ execCommand。

Consumers (planned)採用頁（規劃中）: product-detail.html · bundle-detail.html · create-product.html · create-bundle.html — shown inside Listing controls' shop row when "show in shop" is off.—— 在 Listing controls 的顯示列裡，關掉「顯示於商店」時展開。

Also: 另外：media-vault.html — the vault share link on the Access tab (F9, D475, 2026-10-22), copy only, no reset (the spec gives the link no revoke).—— 存取權限分頁的庫房分享連結（F9，D475，2026-10-22），只有複製、沒有重置（規格沒給這條連結撤銷）。

ds-components/link-field.css · js/link-field.js

4.207 Stock allocation

🟠 organism

Purpose

用途

How one product's inventory pool is split across its sales channels (spec §7.14 · D241). Creating a product creates one atom in the pool and one "single sale" channel; every bundle the product joins is another channel. Any channel can lock N units for itself, and a locked channel does not eat the unlocked stock — so every row needs its own sellable count, not one shared number at the top.

一顆商品的庫存池怎麼分給它的販售管道（規格 §7.14 · D241）。建立商品時池裡建一筆商品、電子商店建一個「單售」販售管道；它加入的每個組合包都是另一個販售管道。任何販售管道都可以從池裡鎖定 N 件給自己，而且鎖定的販售管道不吃沒有被鎖定的庫存量——所以每一列都要有自己的可售量，不是最上面擺一個共用數字。

Two things must always be visible, because they are the rules people get wrong: the per-row sellable count (a locked channel that sold its allocation is sold out even with stock left in the pool), and the warning when every channel is locked — then the unlocked remainder has nobody to sell it, and the row says so with a chip.

兩件事一定要看得到，因為那正是最容易搞錯的規則：逐列的可售量（鎖定的販售管道賣完鎖定量就是售罄，即使池裡還有貨），以及所有販售管道都鎖定時的提醒——那時沒有被鎖定的庫存量沒有人能賣，末列用一顆 chip 講清楚。

Rendered preview (the numbers are the spec's own worked example: pool 100, bundle locks 60)

視覺展示 （數字就是規格的時間軸範例：池 100、組合鎖 60）

Product view — one row per channel

商品視角——一列一個販售管道

For developers — classes · code給工程師 — class · 程式碼
.sallocThe table. Column widths are local custom properties (--salloc-lock-col, --salloc-qty-col) so a consumer that needs different widths overrides the variable instead of writing px into a style attribute.表本體。欄寬走本地變數（--salloc-lock-col、--salloc-qty-col），要調整就覆寫變數，不在 style 屬性寫死 px。
.salloc__rowOne channel (or one member in the bundle view). Hairline --border-soft underneath; the last row drops it.一個販售管道（組合視角則是一個成員）。底下一條 --border-soft 細線，最後一列不畫。
--capThe bundle's own hard cap (Limited edition), 2026-09-09. It sits in the table as a row of its own — same reading as a member row: the input goes in the Locked column, the Sellable column says how many that cap can give. Outside the table it read as an unrelated field: set 5, see 1 below, no visible link. The --free row then names which row decided the minimum (stock.capped-by).組合自己的硬上限（限量版本），2026-09-09 新增。它在表裡佔一列，讀法與成員列相同：輸入框在「鎖定量」那一欄，「可售」欄寫出這道上限給得起幾件。擺在表格外面時它讀起來像無關的欄位——設 5、下面顯示 1，看不出關係。末列 --free 接著寫出這個最小值是被誰壓出來的（stock.capped-by）。
--head · --pool · --freeColumn labels, the pool total, and the unlocked remainder. Pool and remainder are separated from the channel rows by a solid --border, because they are the premise and the leftover — not two more channels.欄標題、目前在庫、沒有被鎖定的庫存量。池與沒有被鎖定的庫存量用實線 --border 與販售管道列隔開——它們是前提與剩餘，不是另外兩個販售管道。
.salloc__qtySellable count. Tabular numerals, right aligned: these are read down the column and compared (the bundle's stock is the smallest of them), so they must line up.可售量。等寬數字、靠右：這一欄是要往下掃著比大小的（組合可售量＝取最小值），對不齊就掃不動。
.salloc__chip · .salloc__errorBoth span the full row width on a second line. The chip carries "N unlocked — neither single sale nor bundles can reach them"; the error line carries the over-limit message and matches .field__error in size and colour, because it is the same role.兩者都在第二行橫跨整列。chip 放「N 件未鎖定，沒有販售管道能賣」；錯誤行放超標訊息，字級與顏色比照 .field__error——那是同一個角色。
.salloc__name--thumbName cell carrying the read-only per-combination thumb .variant-thumb--ro before the combination name (2026-09-11 D268; 2026-09-12 Q117: in the cell, not a column). Switches the name cell from stacked to a horizontal row, vertically centred, gap --sp-12. Tracks unchanged.名稱格帶唯讀逐組合縮圖 .variant-thumb--ro、放在組合名前面（2026-09-11 D268；2026-09-12 Q117：併在格內、不另開欄）。名稱格由直排改橫排、垂直置中、間距 --sp-12。欄軌不變。
.salloc--bundleFour columns instead of three: member name, that member's unlocked stock (read-only), the amount locked for this bundle (editable), and what this bundle can therefore sell.四欄而非三欄：成員名、該成員的沒有被鎖定的庫存量（唯讀）、鎖定給本組合的量（可填）、本組合因此能賣幾套。

The numbers come from js/listing-state.js數字全部來自 js/listing-state.js — freeQty(product), channelQty(product, channel), bundleQty(bundle, productsById), allChannelsLocked(product, bundles), and validateLock(product, channel, wanted) for the per-field maximum. Never recompute min(members) or "pool minus locks" in a page.—— freeQty(product)、channelQty(product, channel)、bundleQty(bundle, productsById)、allChannelsLocked(product, bundles)，欄位上限走 validateLock(product, channel, wanted)。頁面不得自己再算一次 min(成員) 或「池減鎖定」。

4.208 Stock history

🟡 molecule

Stock history for one product: restock orders and lock changes in one list, one row per order. The main row says when / which kind / what changed per combination / supplier · method / status; open the row for quantities, supplier, ETA (with Mark received while restocking) and the note. History is mostly scanned and only occasionally inspected, so the main row stays clean. Built on Data list + Row disclosure (Q107 "whole group changes layer"); this file only owns the date column, badge slot, delta digits and the key / value cells of the expanded rows. 2026-09-11, promoted from lab-stock-history.html H1.

一件商品的庫存變動：補貨單與鎖定變動合併一張清單，一張單一筆。主列講什麼時候／哪一種／逐組合變了多少／供應商 · 方式／狀態；點開看逐組合數量、供應商、預計到貨（補貨中時附到貨確認）、備註。歷史紀錄多數時候是掃、偶爾才查細節，主列要乾淨。疊在 Data list＋Row disclosure（Q107 整組換層）上，本檔只擁有日期欄、徽章槽、變動數字與展開層的鍵／值格。2026-09-11 自 lab-stock-history.html H1 promote。

4.209 State check

🟡 molecule

A read-only row of on/off states — a small round mark plus a label per state. It is not an input: the row it sits in is what you click (it takes you to the setting). On = success-tint circle with a check and foreground label; off = hairline circle and muted label. First use (2026-09-11, user: “pull listing / shown / on-sale out of the settings overview, checkbox-like but not necessarily a checkbox”): the “Listing & sale” row of the product and bundle detail overview, driven by the three switches and their schedules.

一排唯讀的開／關狀態——每個狀態一顆小圓標記＋標籤。它不是輸入控件：點的是它所在的整列（前往設定）。開＝成功綠 tint 圓底＋勾、標籤前景色；關＝髮線空心圓、標籤輔助色。首用（2026-09-11 使用者：「商品選單將上架／顯示／開賣拆出來，做成 checkbox 的形式，但設計不一定要用 checkbox」）：商品／組合詳情總覽的「上架與開賣」列，值由三組開關與排程推導。

4.210 Lock sets

🟠 organism

A bundle’s stock lock, expressed in sets: one “Locked sets” field (blank = not locked; capped by the member with the least unlocked stock), then a framed list — one row per item in the bundle with its stock, unlocked count and “locked = N × per set”. Single-option items are read-only; multi-option items expand (row-disclosure) to optional per-option pinning — sum ≤ N × per set, under-pinning just constrains the total. Rule redefined by the user 2026-09-11; visual is the L3 outcome of lab-bundle-lock.html: 1px frame, edge-to-edge hairlines between rows, the expanded group hovers as one, three numeric columns end 12px from the column edge so the input’s unit lines up with the read-only “N pcs”.

組合包的庫存鎖定，以套為單位：一個「鎖定套數」欄位（空白＝不鎖定；上限受未鎖定量最少的那件商品限制），底下一張外框清單——組合內每件商品一列：商品庫存、未鎖定、已鎖定＝N × 每套用量。單一選項商品唯讀；多選項商品可展開（row-disclosure）逐規格選填分配——Σ ≤ N × 用量，少分配＝只限總數。規則由使用者 2026-09-11 重定義；視覺是 lab-bundle-lock.html L3 定案：1px 外框、列間貼邊髮線、展開的整組一起 hover、三個數字欄的墨水距欄右緣 12，輸入框的單位才跟唯讀的「N 件」對齊。

鎖定套數最多 3 套（受「海上霸姬 幕後寫真誌 vol.02」限制）；空白＝不鎖定

4.213 Bundle preview card

🟡 molecule

The fan-facing bundle card that sits in the right column while a creator builds a bundle (event step 6, js/bundle-editor.js layout:'split'). Promoted 2026-09-21 from docs/bundle-create-demo-2026-09-21.html after the user ruled 「make it production」. Composed on Preview card (media, name row, description) plus what a bundle adds: an included list in the same order as the editor’s sections (tickets → items → perks), each line an icon + text, with working pickers — date chips when the event has several dates and the bundle is shared across them, tier chips when more than one tier is allowed, one chip row per product option (size, colour). A chip that can’t be picked (a tier with less than a set left, a sold-out option) is disabled — faded and struck, still in the row. Below: selling price (「from $Y」 until every choice is made, then a single figure), struck list price and a 「N% off」 badge when discounted, 「N sets left」 or a Sold-out badge, and an add-to-cart stand-in that is disabled with a one-line reason (「Pick tier, size first」) until the fan has chosen. Picking here only changes this card, never the form. Media is 2:1, not the preview card's portrait 2:3 — in a 340px rail a portrait image would push the included list below the fold (STYLE-DECISIONS Q120, pending). The fan-side bundle page is not specified upstream (ASSUMPTIONS BDL-001/002): this card is a presentation derivation, not a front-end spec.

創作者建組合包時右欄那張「粉絲看到的」卡（建立活動第 5 步，js/bundle-editor.js 的 layout:'split'）。2026-09-21 由 docs/bundle-create-demo-2026-09-21.html promote，使用者裁示「做上正式」。以 Preview card（媒體格、名稱列、說明）為底，加上組合包多出來的段落：內含清單（順序與編輯器分卡一致：票券 → 商品 → 額外權益），一列 icon ＋ 文字，且選擇器可以點——多場且一組通用時有場次 chip、允許多種票種時有票種 chip、多規格商品每個選項一排 chip（尺寸、顏色）。選不到的 chip（剩餘不足一組的票種、沒貨的規格）標 disabled：淡化＋劃線、仍留在排上。底下是售價（沒選齊寫「從 $Y 起」，選齊落到單一數字）、有折扣時的劃線原價＋「省 N%」徽章、「剩 N 組」或售罄徽章、一顆沒選齊就停用並寫一句理由（「先選票種、尺寸」）的「加入購物車」示意鈕。點選只影響這張卡，不寫回表單。媒體格 2:1、不是 preview-card 的直式 2:3——340px 右欄裡直式圖會把內含清單推到摺線下（STYLE-DECISIONS Q120 待裁決）。粉絲端組合包頁面上游未定案（ASSUMPTIONS BDL-001／002）：這張卡是呈現推導，不是前台規格。

ds-components/bundle-preview-card.css · markup generated by js/bundle-editor.js (fanHTML(), layout:'split') · depends on preview-card, chip (:disabled), badge, button

Anatomy

.bpc__title (rail heading, outside the card) → .preview-card.bpc > .preview-card__media (portrait 2:3 since 2026-10-12, was 2:1) + .preview-card__body > .preview-card__row (__name[.is-empty] + .ztor-badge event status) · .preview-card__desc · .bpc__list > .bpc__row ×N (16px icon + .bpc__what[.is-empty] + .bpc__pick ×N > .bpc__picklabel + .chip-group > .chip[.chip--active][:disabled] + optional trailing .bpc__picklabel note) · .bpc__price > .bpc__now + .bpc__was + .ztor-badge--warning · .bpc__meta (sets left / .ztor-badge--error sold out / per-date note) · .btn.btn--primary.bpc__cta[disabled] · .bpc__why → .bpc__note (outside the card)

Behavior / usage

Chips carry data-bd-fan-sess / data-bd-fan-tier / data-bd-fan-var (+ -opt, -val); the editor keeps the fan's picks in a side map (not on the bundle record) and re-renders the card in place on every form change. Price = picked tier × per-set quantity + each item at its picked option price (lowest while unpicked), then × (1 − discount %). A tier chip is disabled when the picked date has fewer tickets left than one set (D296 rule 4a); an option value is disabled when no variant with that value has stock (D295). CTA disabled until every picker has a choice, or when sellable sets is 0 (「Sold out」). Read-only stand-in: nothing here writes state.

Token usage

--font-display／--fs-14／--muted-foreground (title), --border-soft (list and price dividers), --font-ui／--fs-13／--foreground (what), --fs-11 (pick labels), --font-numeric／--fs-22 (now), --fs-13 (was — same size as .cb-basestrike, Q35 state A), --fs-12 (meta／why／note), --lh-relaxed, --sp-6…14. No colour of its own beyond text tiers — badges and chips bring theirs.

Usage

create-event.html › step 6 › bundle dialog right column (layout:'split', markup by fanHTML()); create-bundle.html › right preview column (2026-09-21 batch 2, page-level: the outer card is the create-page form-section--outlined preview shell per the 2026-09-04 ruling, the card inside is this component, markup by the page’s own fanHTML() with data-cb-fan-tier/data-cb-fan-var chips). bundle-detail.html has no rail — the fan-facing view there stays the See-as-fan shop mirror. Exploration: docs/bundle-create-demo-2026-09-21.html.

4.214 Section tabs

🟡 molecule

A fixed row of section tabs at the top of a long, sectioned form (the bundle editor’s Contents → Pricing & stock → Name & listing): clicking scrolls smoothly to that section, scrolling highlights the section in view (scrollspy), ←／→／Home／End move between tabs. Added 2026-09-21 from the user’s feedback on the “New ticketed product” dialog (“a tab fixed at the top of the popup that jumps to a section”); the shop’s Create bundle page shares the same mental model, so one component serves both. Not a panel switcher (that is Tabs + .tab-panel) and not Section nav (a detail page’s left rail that swaps panels) — all sections stay on screen; the row only jumps and tracks. Only one fixed row (the spirit of STYLE-DECISIONS Q115): both consumers already have a fixed header, so the row joins that header as its second line — --dialog sits between .payout-dialog__head and __body (outside the scroller), --wizard sits inside .wizard__top after the top bar. Nothing else floats over the content, so scroll offsets need no correction and the right-column preview card’s sticky is never covered. The shell is the 44px “shell” the Q38 underline recipe requires: items stretch to the shell height so the glowing underline lands on its bottom edge, exactly as in List toolbar.

一頁很長、分成幾段的表單（組合包編輯器的「內容物 → 定價與庫存 → 命名與上架」）頂端的一排固定分節分頁：點了平滑捲到那一段、捲動時亮著目前看到的那一段（scrollspy）、←／→／Home／End 在分頁間移動。2026-09-21 由使用者對「新增票務商品」彈窗的反饋而來（「popup 中要有 fix 在最上面的 tab 可以快速滾動到指定區塊」）；電子商店的建立組合頁是同一個心智模型，同一支元件裝兩處。不是切換面板的分頁（那是 Tabs＋.tab-panel），也不是 Section nav（詳情頁左側、切換面板）——三段都在畫面上，這一排只負責跳轉與定位。只准一條固定列（STYLE-DECISIONS Q115 的精神）：兩個消費處都已經有固定頂列，這一排併進那條頂列當第二排——--dialog 放在 .payout-dialog__head 與 __body 之間（捲動區外）、--wizard 放在 .wizard__top 的頂列之後。沒有別的東西浮在內容上，捲動偏移不必修正、右欄預覽卡的 sticky 也不會被蓋到。殼就是 Q38 底線配方要求的 44px「殼」：item 撐滿殼高，發光底線落在殼的下緣，與 List toolbar 同一個機制。

In context · dialog head + section tabs + scrolling body (the tabs work — click or scroll the box)實際情境 · 彈窗標題列＋分節分頁＋會捲的內容區（分頁可以點、方框可以捲）

New bundle新增組合包

Contents內容物

What a fan gets in one set.粉絲買一組會拿到什麼。

Tickets, items, perks…票券、商品、額外權益…

Pricing & stock定價與庫存

What it sells for, and how many sets.賣多少、最多賣幾組。

List total, discount, locked sets, cap…原價合計、折扣、鎖定套數、上限…

Name & listing命名與上架

The name fans see, and when they see it.粉絲看到的名字，以及什麼時候看得到。

Name, description, cover, follows the event.名稱、說明、封面、跟著活動。

Spec

Source

ds-components/section-tabs.css (shell + two placement modifiers) · js/section-tabs.js (window.ZtorSectionTabs.init(root?) / .sync(root?)) · depends on tabs (--underline-short + --underline-label)

Anatomy

nav.section-tabs[data-section-tabs] (shell: height: var(--control-h-md) 44px, display:flex; align-items:stretch, flex:none) > .tabs.tabs--underline-short.tabs--underline-label > button.tabs__item[data-st-tab="key"] > <span> label; the current one carries .tabs__item--active + aria-current="location". Targets inside the scroller: [data-st-section="key"]. Modifiers: .section-tabs--dialog (padding --sp-6/--sp-20 so labels share the title’s left edge, 1px --border bottom rule), .section-tabs--wizard (max-width 1000 + --sp-28 − --sp-14 padding = the .wizard__body--narrow content column).

Behavior / usage

data-st-scroller (optional) names the scroll container (nav.closest → sibling query → document; default = nearest scrollable ancestor, else window). data-st-offset (optional, comma list) names fixed bars covering the scroller’s top edge (create-bundle passes .wizard__top); jumps and the spy line subtract their bottom edge, and a nav that sits inside the scroller counts as one. Click: target = section top − offset − 16px, scrollTo({behavior:'smooth'}) (auto under reduced motion). While the smooth scroll travels the spy yields, released on arrival (|scrollTop − target| ≤ 2, bottom reached, or scrollend) with a 2.5s safety — not a fixed delay, because smooth-scroll duration is not guaranteed (a background tab even delays the start) and a timer that fires mid-flight snaps the active tab back to a section passed on the way. Spy: rAF-throttled scroll listener, current = the last section whose top ≤ offset + 17px; at the bottom of the scroller the last section wins so a short final section can be reached. Keyboard: ←/→ (wrap) and Home/End move focus; Enter/Space are native <button>. Idempotent via data-st-ready; hosts that re-render (bundle-editor swaps innerHTML on every render()) call init() again. On init it writes its own height to --section-tabs-h on the scroller (or <html>) so sibling sticky elements can add it — create-bundle: .preview-split { --preview-col-top: calc(96px + var(--section-tabs-h, 0px)) }.

Token usage

--control-h-md (shell height), --sp-6／--sp-20／--sp-28／--sp-14 (padding), --border (dialog bottom rule), --foreground (current label). Type, underline and glow come from Tabs’ --underline-short／--underline-label; nothing is redeclared here.

Usage

create-event.html › step 6 “New bundle” dialog (js/bundle-editor.js sectionTabsHTML(), layout:'split' only; the three .bd-sec--stack sections carry data-st-section); create-bundle.html › second row of .wizard__top (three form-section--outlined.cb-sec carry data-st-section). The funding pages (layout:'sections') have no such row. Do: join an existing fixed header; keep tab labels and section titles on the same i18n keys (cpp.bd.sp.sec.*). Don’t: use it to switch panels; don’t drop bare .tabs--underline-short into a header without this 44px shell (Q38).

4.215 Fan event page

🟠 organism

The fan-facing full event page used as the publish-preview / preview-and-localize content for events (D305, 2026-09-22). 2026-09-22 user ruling: “the preview UI must look exactly like these two pages — only the data and mechanics follow our flow” (ticket page shop-item.html?id=ev-score-live, ticket-bundles page shop-item.html?id=ev-themesong-night&sets=1). So this is no longer a structural re-draw in this site’s tokens: js/fan-event-page.js emits the front end’s DOM and class names (evidence: docs/fe-mirror-2026-09-22/ticket.main.html / bundles.main.html), and ds-components/fan-shop.css is every CSS rule those two pages actually hit, extracted by Playwright and scoped under .fep-shop (its :root tokens included). Only the data changes — name, highlight, description, lineup, notes, ticket delivery, ticket types, bundles (allowed tiers × qty, goods, perks, price / list price) all come from our create-event flow or events-store; purchase buttons are inert; the front end’s “scenario” fab and bottom sheet are not drawn; the component supports three related-event cards flagged “Not this event”, but since 2026-09-23 both hosts pass related:false and the section is not drawn (B feedback, same round as Fan product page).

活動的發布前預覽／預覽與在地化用的粉絲視角完整頁（D305，2026-09-22）。使用者 2026-09-22 裁決：「預覽 UI 必須做得和這兩個一模一樣，只是資料和機制套用我們的流程與功能」（票券頁 shop-item.html?id=ev-score-live、票務商品頁 shop-item.html?id=ev-themesong-night&sets=1）。所以它不再是用站上 token 重刻的結構鏡像：js/fan-event-page.js 輸出的是前台的 DOM 與 class 名（證據：docs/fe-mirror-2026-09-22/ticket.main.html／bundles.main.html），ds-components/fan-shop.css 是那兩頁實際命中的全部 CSS 規則（Playwright 抽出、加 .fep-shop 前綴，含前台的 :root token）。只有資料換成我們的——名稱、亮點、描述、卡司、須知、取票方式、票種、組合包（允許票種 × 張數、商品、權益、售價／原價）全部來自建立活動流程或 events-store；購買類按鈕不動作；前台的「情境展示」浮鈕與底部抽屜不畫；元件支援相關活動三張卡標「非本活動」，但 2026-09-23 起兩個宿主都改傳 related:false、該段不畫（B 反饋，與 Fan product page 同輪）。

Exception (token discipline): fan-shop.css is a front-end mirror, not a component of this design system — it carries the front end’s raw rgb() values and px type sizes on purpose and must not be referenced by any other Creator Studio page. Its raw-color count is registered by hand in ds-baseline.json; when the front end changes, re-extract and overwrite the file instead of hand-tuning it.

例外（token 紀律）：fan-shop.css 是前台鏡像、不是本 design system 的元件——它刻意保留前台的裸 rgb() 與 px 字級，且不得被 Creator Studio 其他頁引用。它的裸色數量已手動登記在 ds-baseline.json；前台改了就重抓覆蓋，不在這裡手改。

2026-09-29 (D334): notes / bring and the whole “Good to know” section are gone — the lists were deleted with “Advanced details”; how fans see the new info sections is still open, so the page does not draw them yet (ASSUMPTIONS UIA-186). 2026-09-29 model changes (D327 / D328): “Good to know” merges only What to bring + Good to know (the first list and the ticket-collection note were deleted, includes / entryNote are no longer read); language may be an array (event languages are multi-select — joined on one line); tiers[].desc is the ticket description (translatable, shown under the ticket name); tiers[].hidden: true leaves the ticket out of the price list, the price range and the “N ticket types” count — the demo below carries a hidden third tier that does not render.

2026-09-29（D334）：notes／bring 與整個「注意事項」一節退場——兩份清單隨「進階詳細資料」刪除；新的說明區塊在粉絲頁怎麼呈現尚未決定，本頁暫不畫（ASSUMPTIONS UIA-186）。2026-09-29 model 變更（D327／D328）：注意事項只收需攜帶物品＋活動須知（第一組清單與取票說明欄已刪，不再讀 includes／entryNote）；language 可給陣列（活動語言複選，串成一行）；tiers[].desc＝門票簡介（可翻譯，畫在票名下）；tiers[].hidden: true 的票不在票價清單、價格區間與「N 種票」裡出現——下方示範帶了一張隱藏的第三種票，畫面上不會出現。

2026-10-16（D443）：排版看預覽區自己的寬度（@container fep，斷點照前台 640／768／1024）；寬 ≥1024 右側欄 sticky（上緣 --fep-stick-top，宿主 publish-stage.css 給）；<768 換手機版並藏購票方案大圖卡。「看詳情 ›」（套組卡與大圖卡標題）開彈窗：✕、點遮罩、Esc 關（翻譯表抽屜開著時 Esc 先關抽屜）；「購買套組」「查看單品」不動作；宿主重畫（切語言／幣別）時同一組照樣開著；彈窗帶 data-pp-no-zone，Publish stage 的「編輯」浮鈕不會在裡面出現。價格由宿主算好「開賣當下」交進來（tiers[].offer、bundles[].offer；組合包另設開賣時間時以組合包自己的開賣時間判斷）。 兩個宿主（create-event.html 發布前、event-detail.html 的「預覽與在地化」）把各自資料整理成同一份 model（結構見 js/fan-event-page.js 檔頭）交給本檔渲染。可翻譯欄位由宿主給 { key }，本檔以 api.getValue(key) 取當前語系並掛 api.bindEditable；價格由 api.money(priceObj, priceKey) 依所選幣別格式化，再補成前台的「NT$ 1,200」（符號後一個空格）；區間「NT$ 1,200 – 2,800」高價端不重複符號。前台固定文案（票價／日期／場館／購票／組合方案／退換票那句…）走 studio i18n（en／zh）、跟介面語言；內容欄跟預覽語系；預覽語系是英文時 .fep-shop[lang=en] 整頁換 Satoshi／Inter（前台 html[lang=en] 的同一條規則）。票券頁／票務商品頁切換＝ .pp-fan-viewbar 的 Segmented（CS 控件、畫在頁外）與頁內「只買票 ›」；「N 種票 ›」捲到票種清單、「N 種組合 ›」捲到組合方案。購買類按鈕 data-pp-inert（不動作、不可聚焦）。相關活動用 relatedFromStore(excludeId) 從 events-store 撈三場別的活動（宿主呼叫、本檔不主動讀 store），每張標「非本活動」。

Token usage

全部是前台自己的 token（--bg-primary／--text-*／--border-*／--yellow-500／--font-cjk-display／--fs-*／--space-*／--radius-*／--glass-*／--lg-*），定義在 fan-shop.css 最前面的 .fep-shop { … }，與本站 _tokens.css 不混用；裸 rgb()／px 是前台鏡像的必要例外（見上）。

Consumers

D445（2026-10-19）：model 的 venue、bundles[].name、bundles[].desc 改可給 { key }（吃翻譯表譯文）；新增 meet（集合地點）、transit（交通說明），有值才畫、放在「活動地點」一節的地圖下方，沿用 .pdp-event-meta 的框與列（.pdp-event-meta--venue 只調上緣間距）；組合包成員票券的 tickets.whenBase 由本檔接上場地名稱譯文。

4.243 Fan phone (create-flow phone mirror)

🟡 molecule

Purpose

用途

2026-10-28 (#394, user: “the narrowest design we have is the phone layout — the product preview and event preview in the create flows’ right rail should both look like it”). The event preview on create-event steps 2/3/4/7 is no longer the small Event preview card: it is the phone layout of the fan event page, drawn by the same renderer as step 8 (Fan event page) and set up exactly like create-product’s PDP preview — 270px rail, an outlined form section with the title inside, the page zoomed to 3/4. This frame only shrinks the page; the page switches itself to its phone layout through its own container query.

2026-10-28（UI-CHANGES 394，使用者：「現在最窄的設計就是手機版，右側欄的商品預覽、活動預覽都要做得像他一樣的設計」）。建立活動第 2／3／4／7 步的活動預覽不再是小張的 Event preview card，改成粉絲端活動頁的手機版：與第 8 步同一支渲染器（Fan event page），擺法與建立商品的 PDP preview 完全相同——270px 右欄、標題在卡內的 outlined 區塊、整頁 zoom 3/4。本框只負責縮小；粉絲頁靠自己的容器查詢切成手機版型。

2026-10-28 (UI-CHANGES 397, user: “1 and 2 weren’t changed — and no outline” / “3: no outline”). create-product (replacing the simplified PDP preview) and create-bundle (replacing the bundle preview card) now use this frame too, each drawing its own fan page with the same renderer as its “Preview & publish” step. All three drop the outlined section: the .preview-col__head title and the frame sit straight in the right rail, now a full 270px (= the 360px phone layout at 3/4). The stand-in api and redraw timing live in partials/fan-phone.js, shared by all three hosts.

2026-10-28（UI-CHANGES 397，使用者：「1,2,沒改啊，另外不用外匡」「3,不用外匡」）。建立商品（取代簡化的 PDP preview）與建立組合包（取代組合包預覽卡）也改用本框，各自畫「預覽與發布」那一步同一支渲染器的粉絲頁。三處都拿掉 outlined 外框：.preview-col__head 標題與本框直接放在右欄，框寬因此是完整的 270（＝360 手機版型 × 3/4）。替身 api 與重畫時機收進 partials/fan-phone.js，三個宿主共用。

4.216 Publish stage

🟠 organism

D310 (2026-09-22): “look at it once before publishing” is no longer a modal — it is a full-page screen. In a multi-step flow it is the last step (create-event step 7, “Preview & publish”); after publishing it re-opens as its own page (event-localization.html). Two columns: the fan-view preview (Fan event page) on the left, and a sticky control sidebar on the right with two sections — Language (four locales, each row carrying its state: Default / Auto-translated / N edited; click to switch the preview locale; “Restore auto-translation”; “Field-by-field” opens the translation table) and a second section that depends on mode: 'publish' shows the pre-publish checks (each item jumps back to its step), 'save' shows an unsaved-changes summary. The primary action (Publish event / Save) sits at the bottom of the sidebar; pressing it publishes — there is no second confirmation. Hovering a preview block floats an “Edit” button that jumps back to the step that owns it (editZones).

D310（2026-09-22）：「發布前看一次」不再是浮層，是全頁畫面。多步驟流程裡它就是最後一步（建立活動第 7 步「預覽與發布」）；發布後再開是一個獨立頁（event-localization.html）。兩欄——左邊粉絲視角預覽（Fan event page），右邊 sticky 控制側欄兩段：語言（四語系，每列標狀態：預設／自動翻譯／已修改 N 欄，點選切換預覽語系；有「還原自動翻譯」；「逐欄位對照」開翻譯表）、第二段看 mode：'publish' 是發布前檢核（每項可點跳回該步驟）、'save' 是未儲存的變更摘要。主鈕（發布活動／儲存）在側欄底部，按下即發布、不再問第二次。滑到預覽的某一塊會浮出「編輯」鈕，跳回擁有那塊內容的步驟（editZones）。

2026-10-16 (D443): .pstage__main also sets --fep-stick-top (from --pstage-top) so the fan event page’s sticky side column and set-details dialog sit under the top bar; the hover “Edit” button stays hidden inside anything marked data-pp-no-zone (the dialog). The views option has no consumer any more.

2026-10-16（D443）：.pstage__main 另給 --fep-stick-top（取自 --pstage-top），粉絲活動頁的右側欄與看詳情彈窗靠它黏在頂列下緣；hover「編輯」浮鈕在帶 data-pp-no-zone 的區域（看詳情彈窗）裡不出現。views 選項已沒有消費者。

Why not the wizard rail: .wizard-split__rail is a read-only summary rail; this sidebar is a control panel — every row is clickable, it carries the primary action, and it also has to work on a page with no wizard around it. The sticky offset comes from --pstage-top so the wizard host can set it to its top-bar height without hard-coding a number.

為什麼不是 wizard 的右軌：.wizard-split__rail 是唯讀的摘要軌；這條側欄是控制台——每一列可點、帶主動作，而且在沒有 wizard 的獨立頁也要成立。sticky 位移走 --pstage-top，讓 wizard 宿主把它設成頂列高度，不寫死數字。

Rendered preview (mounted live by js/publish-stage.js with the same demo data as §4.130 — mode:'publish', three-section sidebar, both drawers work)

視覺展示 （由 js/publish-stage.js 用與 §4.130 相同的示範資料當場掛載——mode:'publish'、側欄兩段，兩個抽屜都可開）

Spec

Source

ds-components/publish-stage.css ＋ js/publish-stage.js（window.ztorPublishStage.mount(host, opts)）· consumers: 消費頁：create-event.html（第 8 步，mode:'publish'，translation:'manual'）、event-localization.html（mode:'save'，translation:'manual'）、create-product.html（「開始販售」驗證通過後的全頁畫面，mode:'publish'，未傳 translation＝'auto'）、create-bundle.html（「下一步：預覽」驗證通過後的全頁畫面，D321，mode:'publish'，未傳 translation＝'auto'）、product-localization.html（商品／組合商品細節頁「預覽與在地化」的全頁版，D319，mode:'save'，未傳 translation＝'auto'；?type=bundle 時同一頁改畫組合包）、create-project.html（最後一步「預覽與發布」，D322，mode:'publish'，primaryKey 依 isFilmFlow() 切送出審核／發布項目，未傳 translation＝'auto'）、publish-work.html（作品上架最後一步「預覽與送審」，D323，mode:'publish'，primaryKey＝pw.submit〔送出審核〕，未傳 translation＝'auto'〔基準＝作品自己的定價幣別 F14〕，previewRender＝ Fan project page 作品模式）· depends on 相依 Fan event page（create-event.html／event-localization.html 的 previewRender）、Fan product page（create-product.html 的 previewRender）、Fan bundle page（create-bundle.html 的 previewRender）、Fan project page（create-project.html／publish-work.html 的 previewRender）、Drawer（--full，2026-09-23 起：翻譯表的右側滑出面板；--wide 已無消費者）、Publish preview（表格家族 .pp-table／.pp-cell／.pp-slot--editable／.pp-fan-viewbar，消費頁要一併連 publish-preview.css）、Readiness（檢核列與 banner）、Button、Badge、Table。

Anatomy

.pstage（兩欄 grid）> .pstage__main（粉絲視角預覽，浮動 .pstage-edit 以本欄定位；hover 中的區塊加 .pstage-zone--hot＝品牌橘外框＋淡橘底，2026-09-23）＋ .pstage__side（sticky 卡）> .pstage__side-inner（可捲）> .pstage-sec ×2 > .pstage-sec__title（群組小字標）＋ ul.pstage-list > li > button.pstage-row[.pstage-row--active] > __name ＋ __state；段底 .pstage-sec__foot（還原自動翻譯或清除譯文／逐欄位對照）；檢核段 .pstage-check 沿用 .readiness，每列的字換成 button.pstage-linkbtn；未儲存的變更段 .pstage-sum > __row（__k／__v）或 .pstage-sum__none；底部 .pstage__foot（主鈕＋次鈕，各佔滿一列）。翻譯表掛在 body 上的 .drawer.drawer--full（2026-09-23 起整頁）。

Behavior

宿主用 mount(host, { fields, baseCurrency, views, previewRender, mode, translation, editZones, onEdit, checks, onPrimary, onSecondary }) 掛載，拿到 { refresh, syncChecks, result, unmount }。翻譯草稿（DRAFTS／EDITED）是模組變數、不隨每次 mount 重置——回去改一個字再回來，改過的翻譯都還在。價格不存草稿，每次由宿主的 priceObj 即時換算（D514）。預覽語系預設跟宿主走（介面語言），使用者點過才固定；預覽價格固定用創作者幣別（宿主給的 baseCurrency），沒有幣別切換，其他幣別的換算價在粉絲端才出現（D514 決定三）。預設語系那一欄唯讀（母本回表單改，D223），其餘語系在預覽裡就地 contenteditable。價格只看不改（D514）：預覽裡的價格不可點，要改回原表單的基準價。onPrimary 收到 { translations, mode }；mode:'publish' 時檢核沒全過，主鈕 disabled。

Translation mode (D312, 2026-09-23)

translation: 'auto' | 'manual'，選填、預設 'auto'。auto（原行為）：切到還沒填的語系時，草稿直接鏡射預設語言目前值——等於自動翻譯，讀者分不出是原文還是已確認的譯文；create-product.html、create-project.html 未傳此選項，沿用這個模式。manual：其他語系不把原文抄進草稿（未填＝空字串），預覽與翻譯表改用原文當 fallback 顯示（畫面上看得到內容、草稿仍是空）；側欄語言列狀態字串改「未翻譯 Not translated」／「已翻譯 n of N translated」（n＝已填欄數、N＝翻譯表列數）；翻譯表未填格加原文當 placeholder（[data-stage-drawer="trans"] .pp-cell-field::placeholder）；還原鈕文案由「還原自動翻譯」換成「清除譯文 Clear translation」，行為也從「回填原文」改成「清空該語系草稿」。create-event.html 與 event-localization.html 掛 translation:'manual'（活動內容需要人工確認翻譯，主規格 §7.4「活動例外：手動翻譯」）。兩種模式共用同一個 result() 結構，差別只在「空字串」會不會被自動填滿。

Currency (D514, 2026-11-05, retires D306 / D318 overrides)

fields[].locked（D445，2026-10-19）：bookyay 匯入、原文鎖定的列（描述、條款及細則、場地名稱、集合地點、交通說明、票種名稱；活動名稱不鎖）在欄位名後標小鎖頭 .pp-field-lock（role="img"、title／aria-label＝i18n pp.field.locked）。原文欄本來就唯讀、譯文欄照常可填；翻譯表另有 venue／venue-meet／venue-transit／session-<id>／bundle-name-<id>／bundle-desc-<id> 六組 key，粉絲頁預覽用同一組 key 取譯文。

墓碑 2026-11-06（D517 決定六）：priceObj.pin 與 bkyBundleHkd() 退場。bookyay 票價以創作者幣別帶入，直接是基準價；含 bookyay 票券的組合包照一般組合包，其他幣別一律換算。

墓碑 2026-11-05（D514）：價格表抽屜、priceOverrides 選項、prices[i].overridable 列級旗標、priceObj.override／baseOpen、預覽價格的 data-fep-price-key 全部退場。五個建立流程與兩個在地化頁的預覽一律只看不改。墓碑 2026-11-05（D514 決定三）：側欄「幣別」段（基準／換算／bookyay 原價標記）、預覽幣別切換、currencies 與 prices 選項、.pstage-row__name--code、i18n pstage.sec.currency／pstage.cur.* 全部退場。

Do / Don’t

Do: Do：把「發布前看一次」做成流程的最後一步或一個獨立頁，讓預覽拿到整個寬度。
Don’t: Don’t：在這個畫面之後再加一層「確認發布」浮層——本畫面就是那層確認（D310）。
Don’t: Don’t：把「編輯」鈕插進粉絲頁的 markup——那份 markup 是前台 1:1 鏡像，塞東西進去就不再是鏡像，可翻譯欄位又是 contenteditable，塞在裡面會被當成內容編輯；改用浮動的 .pstage-edit。

4.217 Calc list

🟡 molecule

Purpose

用途

A list of amounts that add up or subtract down to a result. The operator sits in its own leading column and the result row lives inside the same box, so “this number is those two added together” is something you can see rather than work out. 2026-09-22 (D311) promoted from bundle-editor.css’s .bd-calc-list: the single-ticket dialog’s “Price & quantity” block and the bundle editor’s “Full price” are the same visual role, and the site does not keep two answers for one role (.bd-calc-list/__how/__total stay there as a migration alias — bundle-detail.html's pricing card is owned by another session this round and did not switch class; this component is canonical, and the alias goes when that page migrates). The --form variant lets rows carry inputs — the ticket dialog’s price and booking fee are typed into the list, while fans-pay, platform fee and you-receive are read-only cells that recompute as you type.

一組彼此有加減關係的金額，最後一列是結果。運算子自己站一欄、結果列坐在同一個框裡，「這個數字是那兩個加出來的」用看的就成立；做成一欄一欄的獨立欄位時，關係只存在讀者的腦子裡。2026-09-22（D311）自 bundle-editor.css 的 .bd-calc-list promote——單張門票彈窗的「價格與數量」與組合包的「原價合計」是同一個視覺角色，站上不留兩份做法（那三個 class 在 bundle-editor.css 暫留成遷移別名——bundle-detail.html 的定價卡本輪由另一個 session 持有、沒有跟著改 class；canonical 是本元件，該頁遷移的那一輪連同別名一起刪）。--form 變體讓列裡可以放輸入控件——門票彈窗的票價直接打在列上，粉絲付／平台費／創作者實收是唯讀格、邊打邊重算。2026-09-23（D316）門票那一組收成四列：平台費與支付費一律由創作者負擔，票價之上沒有加給粉絲的手續費，所以「＋ 手續費」那一列退場，粉絲付＝票價。

2026-10-15 — mobile: at ≤480px the --form value column shrinks from 168px to 112px (62px currency code + four digits), so the left column fits a whole source tag and the input no longer runs past the frame; a source tag in the left column drops its left margin to line up with the label.

2026-10-15 手機：≤480px 時 --form 的右欄由 168px 收成 112px（幣別代碼 62px＋4 位數），左欄放得下一整顆來源標記、輸入框不再超出外框；左欄裡的來源標記去掉左外距、跟欄名左緣對齊。

2026-10-15 — mobile, revised: 112px left only 38px for digits after the 62px currency code and 12px right padding, so a four-digit price “2100” showed as “210”. The value column is now 124px and an .amount-field--code input inside it pads 54px left / 8px right (the code box is ~46px, so an 8px gap remains) — 62px of digits, room for seven.

2026-10-15 手機修訂：112px 扣掉幣別代碼 62px 與右內距 12px，數字只剩 38px，4 位數票價「2100」被裁成「210」。右欄改 124px，其中 .amount-field--code 輸入框內距收成左 54／右 8（代碼盒約 46px，仍留 8px 空隙）——數字區 62px，放得下 7 位數。

Relationships

In context

實際情境

The whole block above is how the single-ticket dialog’s “Price & quantity” section reads (create-event step 4 / event-detail Tickets, D311; the booking-fee row retired with D316 on 2026-09-23). Quantity deliberately sits outside the list: an operator column next to a non-money field would read as if it took part in the sum.右邊整塊就是單張門票彈窗「價格與數量」區的樣子（建立活動第 4 步／活動詳情票務，D311；2026-09-23 D316 收掉手續費那一列）。數量刻意排在列表之外：運算子欄旁邊放一個不是金額的欄位，會讓人以為它也參與計算。
Spec

Source

ds-components/calc-list.css · consumers: 消費頁：create-event.html（單張門票彈窗）、event-detail.html（票種彈窗）、create-bundle.html 與 js/bundle-editor.js（原價合計）· needs 相依 KV list（.kv 的列版面，消費頁要一併連 kv-list.css）。

Anatomy

.calc-list[.calc-list--form][.calc-list--framed]（1px --layer-line、--radius-lg；坐在卡片式區塊內預設去框，--framed＝線框表格，比照組合包價格（.price-ladder）：淡外框、列線貼齊兩緣、列內距 14/16、項目名前景色、金額 16px，卡內也畫框；用於票種彈窗「價格與數量」拆成的價格算式、數量兩張表格）> .kv > .kv__k（含列首的 .calc-list__op 運算子欄與 .calc-list__how 這一項怎麼算）＋ .kv__v（--font-numeric、tabular-nums）；小計列 .calc-list__sum、結果列 .calc-list__total（值升到 --fs-16）。唯讀格 .calc-list__ro 在 --form 下補 --sp-10 右內距，數字才與輸入框裡的文字同一條線；--form 右欄 168px（2026-10-12 由 140 加寬，票價前綴改幣別代碼）。次要層 .calc-list__sub（只在 --framed 內有樣式，2026-10-12）：包住算出來的列（平台費、創作者實收），淡壓暗底（L0 檯面 12%）、上緣一條貼齊兩緣的列線、框內列與列之間的線左右各縮進 --sp-16、13px、墨色 --foreground-muted。

Do / Don’t

Do: Do：只在金額之間真的有運算關係時用。
Do: Do：一個框裡只放大一次值（結果列）——放大兩次就分不出誰是最後一個數字。
Don’t: Don’t：拿它當唯讀事實清單用，那是 KV list 的工作。
Don’t: Don’t：把不是金額的欄位（數量、日期）塞進來。

4.218 Fan product page

🟠 organism

The fan-facing full product page used as create-product’s publish-preview content (D310 decision 4, 2026-09-23) — the product-side sibling of Fan event page, built the same way: a 1:1 mirror of the front-of-house shop-item page’s goods branch, not a structural redraw in this site’s tokens. js/fan-product-page.js reads the front end’s source (assets/shop-detail-render.js’s renderGoods() / goodsBuyHtml() / priceBlock() / variantsHtml() / qtyHtml() / deliveryMicroHtml() / stockLineHtml() / detailsSectionHtml() / relatedHtml() / buyBarHtml()) and emits the matching DOM and class names; ds-components/fan-shop.css section 4 (“product-page components”) extends the .fep-shop tokens section 1–3 already established for the event mirror. Only the data changes — name, gallery, category, options (colour / size), price, discount, stock, specs, pickup & returns all come from the create-product form; purchase buttons are inert; the component supports four related-product cards flagged “Not this item”, but since 2026-09-23 create-product.html passes related:false and the section is not drawn (B feedback: “remove ‘you may also like’”).

建立商品「開始販售」發布前預覽用的粉絲視角完整商品頁（D310 決定四，2026-09-23）——Fan event page 的商品版，做法一樣：前台 shop-item 頁商品分支的 1:1 鏡像，不是用站上 token 重刻的結構鏡像。js/fan-product-page.js 讀前台原始碼（assets/shop-detail-render.js 的 renderGoods()／goodsBuyHtml()／priceBlock()／variantsHtml()／qtyHtml()／deliveryMicroHtml()／stockLineHtml()／detailsSectionHtml()／relatedHtml()／buyBarHtml()）輸出對應的 DOM 與 class 名；ds-components/fan-shop.css 第 4 段「商品頁元件」延續活動鏡像已經建好的第 1–3 段 .fep-shop token。只有資料換成我們的——名稱、圖庫、分類、選項（顏色／尺寸）、價格、折扣、庫存、規格、取貨與退貨全部來自建立商品表單；購買類按鈕不動作；元件支援相關商品四張卡標「非本商品」，但 2026-09-23 起 create-product.html 改傳 related:false、該段不畫（B 反饋「你可能也喜歡都移除」）。

Exception (token discipline): same registered exception as Fan event page §4.215 — fan-shop.css is a front-end mirror, not a component of this design system, and must not be referenced by any other Creator Studio page. Section 4 adds no new raw-color exception; its count is folded into the file’s existing ds-baseline.json registration.

例外（token 紀律）：與 Fan event page §4.215 同一則已註記的例外——fan-shop.css 是前台鏡像、不是本 design system 的元件，不得被 Creator Studio 其他頁引用。第 4 段沒有新增裸色例外，數量併入該檔既有的 ds-baseline.json 登記。

Rendered preview (rendered live by js/fan-product-page.js with a demo model and a stub api — same code path as create-product.html)

視覺展示 （由 js/fan-product-page.js 用示範 model 與替身 api 當場渲染——與 create-product.html 同一條路徑）

Spec

Source

ds-components/fan-shop.css（.fep-shop 第 4 段，前台鏡像）· markup 由 js/fan-product-page.js 的 window.ztorFanProductPage.render(host, model, api) 產生，經 Publish stage 的 previewRender 掛進全頁畫面 · consumers: 消費頁：create-product.html（「開始販售」驗證通過後，related:false）、product-localization.html（細節頁的「預覽與在地化」，D319；?type=bundle 時同一支畫組合包——封面＝組合展示圖、價格＝組合售價、規格表＝內容物一列＋逐項額外權益）· depends on 相依 Publish stage、icon（前台 .ds-icon 改包 lucide）。

Anatomy（＝前台 shop-item 商品分支的 <main>）

.fep-shop > main > .section.section-bg-page.section--flush-top > .pdp[data-type=goods] > .pdp-topbar（手機頂欄，不可點）＋ .pdp__page > .container > .page-head__crumbs（商店 › 分類 › 名稱）＋ .pdp__grid > .pdp-gallery[data-fpp-gallery]（縮圖條＋2:3 主圖，>1 張才有左右箭頭）＋ .pdp-buy > .pdp-buy__meta（選填徽章）＋ h1.pdp-buy__title（可翻譯）＋ .pdp-host--shop（店家列，model.shop 給了才畫）＋ .pdp-price[data-fpp-price]（__now／__was／__save，價格節點帶 data-fpp-price-key）＋ .pdp-stock[data-fpp-stock]（限量且剩 ≤10 件才有，「僅剩 N 件」）＋ .pdp-variants[data-fpp-variants]（.pdp-variant[data-variant=colour|size] ×N：顏色＝.pdp-swatch、其餘＝.pdp-size-chip；.pdp-variant__guide 尺寸指南鈕掛第一個非顏色列，沒有這種列就 .pdp-variant--reflone 自成一列）＋ .pdp-buy__actions（.pdp-qty 數量、.btn--mono.pdp-buy__add 加入購物車／已售完、.pdp-buy__heart 收藏）＋ .pdp-delivery[data-fpp-delivery]（交付方式一行）；section.pdp-details > .pdp-details__inner.container > h2 商品詳情＋ p.pdp-details__lead（描述，可翻譯）＋ .pdp-details__cols > __left（.pdp-details__specs-block[data-fpp-specs] > dl.pdp-specs）＋ __right（.pdp-details__shipping-block[data-fpp-returns]，model.returns 有值才畫）；section.pdp-recs 你可能也喜歡 > a.pdp-rec-card ×4（標「非本商品」）；.shop-buybar（手機底部購買列）。

Behavior

宿主（create-product.html）把表單資料整理成一份 model（結構見 js/fan-product-page.js 檔頭：category／badge／gallery／name／shop／price／variants／discountPct／stock／sizeGuide／delivery／desc／specs／returns／related）交給本檔渲染，本檔只知道「粉絲商品頁長什麼樣」、不知道資料來源。可翻譯欄位（name／desc）由宿主給 { key }，本檔以 api.getValue(key) 取當前語系值並掛 api.bindEditable。單一選項給一個價格，多選項取逐組合最低價、價格不同時加「起」；折扣 discountPct > 0 才畫折後價＋劃線原價＋「省 N」，換算後的幣別也照同比例打折。庫存只在限量且剩 ≤10 件時顯示「僅剩 N 件」，0 件時 CTA 轉「已售完」並停用。選項：顏色畫色票（有色號才填色，沒有退回 --border-strong）、其餘畫尺寸格。價格只看不改（D514）。related 為真時畫相關商品固定四張佔位卡（全部標「非本商品」）；related:false（create-product.html 目前值）或未傳時整段不畫。

Token usage

與 Fan event page 同一批前台 token（定義在 fan-shop.css 最前面的 .fep-shop { … }），第 4 段新增的類別沿用同一批、無新 token；與本站 _tokens.css 不混用。

Consumers

create-product.html（「開始販售」驗證通過後的全頁畫面，previewRender 呼叫 buildFanProductModel()）、product-localization.html（previewRender 呼叫 productModel() 或 bundleModel()，D319）、design-system.html §4.218 就地渲染 demo（替身 api）。

4.219 Fan bundle page

🟠 organism

The fan-facing full bundle page used as create-bundle’s publish-preview content (D321, 2026-09-23, mirrors D317’s move of create-product to a two-step form) — the bundle-side sibling of Fan event page and Fan product page, built the same way: a 1:1 mirror of the front-of-house shop-item page’s two bundle branches, not a structural redraw in this site’s tokens. Bundles split into two kinds by member composition — a goods-only bundle (kind:'goods', front end’s renderBundle()) and a ticket-bearing event set (kind:'eventset', front end’s renderEventSet(), which adds a seat-sections block and an event-info box). js/fan-bundle-page.js reads the front end’s source (assets/shop-detail-render.js’s renderBundle() / bundleBuyHtml() / bundlePickerHtml() / bundleContentsHtml() / renderEventSet() / setCrumbsHtml() / setBuyHtml() / setTiersHtml() / setContentsHtml() / setPerksHtml()) and emits the matching DOM and class names; ds-components/fan-shop.css section 5 (“bundle-page components”) extends the .fep-shop tokens sections 1–4 already established for the event and product mirrors. Only the data changes — name, gallery, members (ticket member’s allowed tiers × qty per set, goods members’ options), selling price, list-price total, bundle discount, sellable sets and bundle-only perks all come from the create-bundle form; purchase buttons are inert; a ticket-bearing bundle does not draw its host event’s own intro / notices / terms (already previewed while creating the event) — the same slot instead draws the bundle’s own description; the component supports four related-item cards, but since 2026-09-23 create-bundle.html passes no related and the section is not drawn (B feedback: “remove ‘you may also like’”, same round as Fan event page / Fan product page).

建立組合「下一步：預覽」後全頁「預覽與發布」用的粉絲視角完整組合包頁（D321，2026-09-23，比照 D317 把建立商品改成兩步驟）——Fan event page／Fan product page 的組合版，做法一樣：前台 shop-item 頁兩個組合分支的 1:1 鏡像，不是用站上 token 重刻的結構鏡像。組合依成員組成分兩種：純商品套組（kind:'goods'，前台 renderBundle()）與含活動票券組合（kind:'eventset'，前台 renderEventSet()，多一段座位區域與活動資訊框）。js/fan-bundle-page.js 讀前台原始碼（assets/shop-detail-render.js 的 renderBundle()／bundleBuyHtml()／bundlePickerHtml()／bundleContentsHtml()／renderEventSet()／setCrumbsHtml()／setBuyHtml()／setTiersHtml()／setContentsHtml()／setPerksHtml()）輸出對應的 DOM 與 class 名；ds-components/fan-shop.css 第 5 段「組合包頁元件」延續活動與商品鏡像已經建好的第 1–4 段 .fep-shop token。只有資料換成我們的——名稱、圖庫、成員（票券成員的允許票種 × 每組張數、商品成員的選項）、售價、原價合計、組合折扣、可售套數、組合專屬權益全部來自建立組合表單；購買類按鈕不動作；含票券組合不畫主辦活動自己的活動介紹／注意事項／購票條款（建立活動時已經預覽過），同一個位置改畫組合自己的說明；元件支援相關推薦四張卡，但 2026-09-23 起 create-bundle.html 不傳 related、該段不畫（B 反饋「你可能也喜歡都移除」，與 Fan event page／Fan product page 同輪）。

Exception (token discipline): same registered exception as Fan event page §4.215 and Fan product page §4.218 — fan-shop.css is a front-end mirror, not a component of this design system, and must not be referenced by any other Creator Studio page. Section 5 adds no new raw-color exception; its count is folded into the file’s existing ds-baseline.json registration.

例外（token 紀律）：與 Fan event page §4.215、Fan product page §4.218 同一則已註記的例外——fan-shop.css 是前台鏡像、不是本 design system 的元件，不得被 Creator Studio 其他頁引用。第 5 段沒有新增裸色例外，數量併入該檔既有的 ds-baseline.json 登記。

Rendered preview (rendered live by js/fan-bundle-page.js with two demo models and a stub api — same code path as create-bundle.html; goods-only bundle on top, ticket-bearing event set below)

視覺展示 （由 js/fan-bundle-page.js 用兩份示範 model 與替身 api 當場渲染——與 create-bundle.html 同一條路徑；上方為純商品套組、下方為含票券組合）

Spec

Source

ds-components/fan-shop.css（.fep-shop 第 5 段，前台鏡像）· markup 由 js/fan-bundle-page.js 的 window.ztorFanBundlePage.render(host, model, api) 產生，經 Publish stage 的 previewRender 掛進全頁畫面 · consumers: 消費頁：create-bundle.html（「下一步：預覽」驗證通過後，未傳 related）· depends on 相依 Publish stage、icon（前台 .ds-icon 改包 lucide）。

Anatomy（＝前台 shop-item 兩個組合分支的 <main>）

.fep-shop[data-fbp-view=bundle|eventset] > main > .section.section-bg-page.section--flush-top > .pdp[data-type=bundle|eventset] > .pdp-topbar（手機頂欄，不可點）＋ .pdp__page > .container > .page-head__crumbs（純商品套組＝商店 › 名稱；含票券組合＝活動 › 活動型態 › 活動名 › 組合名）＋ .pdp__grid > .pdp-gallery[data-fbp-gallery]（組合自己的素材，結構同商品頁縮圖條＋主圖）＋ .pdp-buy > .pdp-buy__meta（狀態／類型徽章）＋ h1.pdp-buy__title（可翻譯）＋ 店家列（goods，.pdp-host--shop）或活動 kicker＋主辦列（eventset，.pdp-set__host）＋（eventset 才有）.pdp-event-meta[data-fbp-meta]（內容／日期／時間／場館／專屬權益逐列）＋ .pdp-price[data-fbp-price]（__now／__was／__save，價格節點帶 data-fbp-price-key）＋ .pdp-stock[data-fbp-stock]（限量且剩 ≤10 組才有，「僅剩 N 組」）＋（goods 才有）.pdp-bundle-picker[data-bundle-picker][data-fbp-picker]（多選項商品成員的規格 chip，不預選）＋ .pdp-buy__actions（eventset＝「購買組合」單鈕；goods＝有選項＝「選擇你的組合」、無選項＝「加入套組」，皆帶數量步進器與收藏鈕；售完＝已售完並停用）；（eventset 才有）section.pdp-bundle--tiers[data-fbp-tiers]「座位區域 · 任選一種」（票券成員允許票種 >1 才畫，每列＝該票種的組合價＋購買鈕）；section.pdp-bundle[data-fbp-members]「套組內容 · 共 N 件」> .pdp-bundle__grid > .pdp-bundle-item ×N（一個成員一張卡：媒體格＋名稱＋規格說明＋查看單品／查看活動連結）；section.pdp-perks[data-fbp-perks]「組合專屬權益 · 共 N 項」> ul.pdp-perks__list > li.pdp-perks__item（星形圖示＋可翻譯文字）；section.pdp-details[data-fbp-desc]「組合介紹」> p.pdp-details__lead（組合自己的說明，可翻譯；model.desc 留空整段不畫）；section.pdp-recs 相關推薦 > a.pdp-rec-card ×4（related 有值才畫，全部標「非本商品／非本活動」）；.shop-buybar（手機底部購買列）。兩種 kind 的區塊順序不同：goods 是套組內容→權益→詳情；eventset 是說明→座位區域→套組內容→權益（前台原始順序）。

Behavior

宿主（create-bundle.html）把表單資料整理成一份 model（結構見 js/fan-bundle-page.js 檔頭：kind／gallery／name／desc／shop／event／members／price／stock／perks）交給本檔渲染，本檔只知道「粉絲組合包頁長什麼樣」、不知道資料來源。可翻譯欄位（name／desc／逐項 perks）由宿主給 { key }，本檔以 api.getValue(key) 取當前語系值並掛 api.bindEditable。members 每筆 { kind:'ticket', qty, tiers } 或 { kind:'product', name, qty, priceObj, options }；票券成員允許票種 >1 時視為「粉絲任選一種」。價格：price.priceObj／price.priceKey 是組合售價（最低組合），price.wasObj 是原價合計（單買加總），price.from 為真時加「起」；座位區域每列的價格＝組合售價＋該票種與最低票種的價差 × 每組張數 × (1 − 組合折扣 %)，差額依匯率換算。庫存 stock 為 null 或空即不限量不顯示，≤10 組才顯示「僅剩 N 組」，0＝售完並停用 CTA。價格只看不改（D514）。related 為真值時畫相關推薦固定四張佔位卡；create-bundle.html 目前不傳時整段不畫。

Token usage

與 Fan event page／Fan product page 同一批前台 token（定義在 fan-shop.css 最前面的 .fep-shop { … }），第 5 段新增的類別沿用同一批、無新 token。

Consumers

create-bundle.html（「下一步：預覽」驗證通過後的全頁畫面，previewRender 呼叫 buildFanBundleModel()）、design-system.html §4.219 就地渲染 demo（替身 api，kind:'goods'／kind:'eventset' 各一份）。

4.220 Fan project page

🟠 organism

The fan-facing full project page used as create-project’s last-step publish-preview content (D322, 2026-09-23, mirrors D310’s full-page move for events and D317/D321’s two-step forms for products/bundles) — the project-side sibling of Fan event page, Fan product page and Fan bundle page, built the same way: a 1:1 mirror of the front-of-house co-creation/pre-order page and the direct-release title page, not a structural redraw in this site’s tokens. Projects split into three kinds by model.kind — direct release (golive, mirrors front end’s title.html), co-creation (fund) and pre-order (preorder, the latter two share front end’s cocreate-project.html). js/fan-project-page.js reads the front end’s source (cocreate-project.html?kind=music&state=active / ?kind=preorder&state=active, and title.html) and emits the matching DOM and class names; ds-components/fan-shop.css section 6 (“project-page components”) extends the .fep-shop tokens sections 1–5 already established for the event, product and bundle mirrors. Only the data changes — name, poster, gallery, keywords, description or story, tiers (backer bundles / pre-order plans), budget allocation and delivery timeline all come from the create-project form; back/pledge buttons are inert; a freshly-created project always renders as “campaign live, 0 backers, 0%”; the component does not draw related creators, comments, the floating pledge bar or a production-progress timeline (the create flow has no such data yet). 2026-09-23 (D323): the work-upload flow (publish-work.html) reuses this component for its own last-step "Preview & submit" — direct-release video works only (model.kind:'golive'). Three optional model fields extend the anatomy for that consumer: model.work (a flag that keeps the work-mode "About this work" section from rendering empty when there is no story or bullet list), model.credits (renders a "Creative team" section when non-empty) and model.clips (renders a "Trailers & clips" section when non-empty). create-project.html passes none of the three; its rendering is unchanged.

建立專案最後一步「預覽與發布」用的粉絲視角完整專案頁（D322，2026-09-23，比照 D310 把活動全頁化、D317／D321 把商品／組合改兩步驟）——Fan event page、Fan product page、Fan bundle page 的專案版，做法一樣：前台共創／預購頁與標題頁的 1:1 鏡像，不是用站上 token 重刻的結構鏡像。專案依 model.kind 分三種：直接發行（golive，鏡像前台 title.html）、共創（fund）與預購（preorder，後兩者共用前台 cocreate-project.html）。js/fan-project-page.js 讀前台原始碼（cocreate-project.html?kind=music&state=active／?kind=preorder&state=active、title.html）輸出對應的 DOM 與 class 名；ds-components/fan-shop.css 第 6 段「專案頁元件」延續活動、商品、組合鏡像已經建好的第 1–5 段 .fep-shop token。只有資料換成我們的——名稱、海報、圖庫、關鍵字、描述或故事、方案（回饋套組／預購方案）、預算分配、交付時程全部來自建立專案表單；支持／購買類按鈕不動作；剛建立的專案一律畫成「計畫進行中、0 人支持、0%」；元件不畫相關創作者、留言討論、浮動支持條與製作進度時間軸（建立流程階段沒有這批資料）。2026-09-23（D323）：作品上架流程（publish-work.html）沿用本元件做自己最後一步的「預覽與送審」——只用於直接發行的影片家族作品（model.kind:'golive'）。為這個消費頁新增三個選填 model 欄位：model.work（布林旗標，作品模式下沒有故事也沒有條列時不畫空的「關於這部作品」段）、model.credits（有值才畫「創作團隊」段）、model.clips（有值才畫「預告與花絮」段）。create-project.html 不傳這三欄，畫面不變。

Exception (token discipline): same registered exception as Fan event page §4.215, Fan product page §4.218 and Fan bundle page §4.219 — fan-shop.css is a front-end mirror, not a component of this design system, and must not be referenced by any other Creator Studio page. Section 6 adds no new raw-color exception; its count is folded into the file’s existing ds-baseline.json registration.

例外（token 紀律）：與 Fan event page §4.215、Fan product page §4.218、Fan bundle page §4.219 同一則已註記的例外——fan-shop.css 是前台鏡像、不是本 design system 的元件，不得被 Creator Studio 其他頁引用。第 6 段沒有新增裸色例外，數量併入該檔既有的 ds-baseline.json 登記。

Rendered preview (rendered live by js/fan-project-page.js with two demo models and a stub api — same code path as create-project.html; direct-release title page on top, co-creation campaign below)

視覺展示 （由 js/fan-project-page.js 用兩份示範 model 與替身 api 當場渲染——與 create-project.html 同一條路徑；上方為直接發行標題頁、下方為共創計畫）

Spec

Source

ds-components/fan-shop.css（.fep-shop 第 6 段，前台鏡像）· markup 由 js/fan-project-page.js 的 window.ztorFanProjectPage.render(host, model, api) 產生，經 Publish stage 的 previewRender 掛進全頁畫面 · consumers: 消費頁：create-project.html（最後一步「預覽與發布」，D322）· depends on 相依 Publish stage、icon（前台 .ds-icon 改包 lucide）。

Anatomy（＝前台共創／預購頁與標題頁的 <main>）

.fep-shop[data-fpj-view=golive|fund|preorder] > main > 共創／預購（.cocreate-detail）：.detail-back（固定頂端返回列，標題頁沒有）＋ .detail-hero（海報為圖片時帶 .detail-hero__bg 模糊背景）> .container.container--wide.detail-hero__inner > .detail-head > .detail-head__poster[data-fpj-poster]（2:3 海報，空時畫圖示佔位）＋ .detail-head__main > .detail-titlebar > h1.detail-title[data-fpj-title]（可翻譯，共創／預購另帶 .cocreate-cat-tag 分類標籤）＋（有「關於共創／預購」連結時）.detail-titlebar__aside＋（model.creator 有值才有）.cocreate-hero__by（發起人，字首圓頭像）＋ .detail-meta.cocreate-hero__status-line（「計畫進行中」＋資訊列）＋（model.keywords 有值才有）.detail-keywords[data-fpj-keywords]＋募資面板 .cocreate-fund[data-fpj-fund]（金額列＋支持人數、募資或預購目標、進度條、倒數與期間說明、「我要支持」主鈕＋預告片鈕＋收藏分享）；.detail-body >（model.tiers 有值才有）方案區 .reward-tiers[data-fpj-tiers] > .reward-tier ×N（名稱／價格／權益清單／支持人數／名額或不限量／「選擇 →」）＋ .detail-section[data-fpj-about]「關於這部作品／關於這些商品」（.cocreate-prose[data-fpj-desc]＋選填的欄位列表）＋ .detail-section「共創計畫說明／預購計畫說明」（.cocreate-prose[data-fpj-story]）＋（model.delivery 有值才有）交付時程 [data-fpj-delivery]（預期交付就地日期欄 [data-fpj-delivery-input]＋選填 .project-timeline 三態里程碑）＋（kind==='fund' 且 model.budget 有值才有）預算分配 .budget-split[data-fpj-budget]（分段長條＋逐分類百分比與換算金額圖例）＋（model.media 有值才有）.detail-section[data-fpj-media]「概念圖／照片／劇照」（.media-grid 最多 4 張）。直接發行（.title-detail）：.detail-hero > .detail-head > .detail-head__poster[data-fpj-poster]＋ .detail-head__main > h1.detail-title[data-fpj-title]＋（model.facts 有值才有）.detail-meta（年份／分級／片長／語言）＋（model.keywords 有值才有）.detail-keywords[data-fpj-keywords]＋ .detail-desc[data-fpj-desc]（可翻譯）＋動作列 .detail-actions[data-fpj-price]（預告片鈕＋價格鈕 .detail-price，價格節點帶 data-fpj-price-key，免費時改「立即觀看」）；.detail-body > .detail-section[data-fpj-about]「關於這部作品」（model.story 有值時 [data-fpj-story] 故事段＋選填欄位列表）＋（model.media 有值才有）.detail-section[data-fpj-media]＋（model.credits 有值才有）[data-fpj-credits]「創作團隊」（逐角色一列，角色名走介面字、人名原樣）＋（model.clips 有值才有）[data-fpj-clips]「預告與花絮」（卡片式縮圖列，有影格用影格、否則退回主視覺、再無留空底＋play 圖示；D323，僅 publish-work.html 這條路徑傳）。

Behavior

宿主（create-project.html／publish-work.html）把表單資料整理成一份 model（結構見 js/fan-project-page.js 檔頭：kind／film／work／name／desc／story／creator／poster／media／hasTrailer／facts／langLabel／langValue／keywords／meta／access／price／fund／delivery／tiers／budget／credits／clips）交給本檔渲染；work／credits／clips 三個選填欄位僅 D323（publish-work.html）傳入，本檔只知道「粉絲專案頁長什麼樣」、不知道資料來源。可翻譯欄位（name／desc／story／逐方案 name／benefits／逐預算分類 label）由宿主給 { key }，本檔以 api.getValue(key) 取當前語系值並掛 api.bindEditable。金額一律用前台「代碼＋空格＋千分位」寫法，不補貨幣符號。共創／預購募資面板固定顯示 0 人支持、0%、進度條 0% 填充——剛建立的專案沒有真實募得數字可畫。交付時程的預期交付日期是本檔唯一的表單控件（原生 <input type="date">），宿主監聽其 change 寫回自己的單一來源並呼叫 stage.refresh()；未填時顯示「必填 · 粉絲在支持前會看到這個日期」。預算分類配色依序輪替站上既有圖表色（不沿用前台固定三色，分類數量與名稱由創作者自填）。價格只看不改（D514）。本檔不畫相關創作者、留言討論、浮動支持條與製作進度時間軸；創作團隊改為選填（model.credits 有值才畫，見上方 Anatomy），create-project.html 不傳、publish-work.html（D323）傳。

Token usage

與 Fan event page／Fan product page／Fan bundle page 同一批前台 token（定義在 fan-shop.css 最前面的 .fep-shop { … }），第 6 段新增的類別沿用同一批、無新 token。

Consumers

create-project.html（最後一步「預覽與發布」驗證通過後的全頁畫面，D322，previewRender 呼叫 buildFanModel()）、publish-work.html（作品上架最後一步「預覽與送審」，D323，previewRender 呼叫同名 buildFanModel()，傳 model.work／model.credits／model.clips）、design-system.html §4.220 就地渲染 demo（替身 api，直接發行與共創各一份；demo 未涵蓋 D323 的三個新欄位）。

4.221 Info sections ✝ retired

⚰️ tombstone

✝ Retired 2026-10-05 (D354). The repeatable “title + text” cards under an event’s description (built 2026-09-29, D334) are gone: the user turned the description into blocks — each description is an untitled block that can be dragged into place, images and videos each take a block of their own, and “Add description” replaces “Add info section”. The title field no longer exists. The card layout (L2 card-in-card, handle | content | remove, half-transparent while dragging) and the drag / arrow-key reordering moved into Rich body; old data is converted by ztorRichBody.migrate() (a title becomes the block’s first line, in bold). info-sections.css and partials/info-sections.js are tombstones; the original rules are in git history.

✝ 2026-10-05 退場（D354）。活動描述下方可重複的「標題＋內文」卡（2026-09-29 D334 建）整組退場：使用者把描述改成「區塊」——每個描述是一個沒有標題、可拖動排序的區塊，圖片與影片各自成一個區塊，「新增描述」取代「新增說明區塊」，標題欄位不再存在。卡片版面（L2 卡中卡、把手｜內容｜刪除、拖動中半透明）與拖動／上下鍵排序搬進 Rich body；舊資料由 ztorRichBody.migrate() 轉換（原標題成為該區塊第一行、設粗體）。info-sections.css 與 partials/info-sections.js 留墓碑，原始規則見 git 歷史。

4.222 Rich body

🟡 molecule

Purpose

用途

An event’s description, built from blocks (D354, 2026-10-05; spec 5.1.6.1 §4.2 F2, 5.1.6.2 F2, main spec §7.10). There are three kinds — text, image and video. No block has a title; every block can be dragged into place and removed, and fans read them in that order. The first text block is the required description: at least one text block must have words in it (images and videos alone don’t count), so the last text block can’t be removed. “Add description” adds a text block. Inserting an image or video from a text block adds it as its own block right after that text block. Text blocks format with bold, italic, link, numbered list, bulleted list and divider; links take http and https only and open in a new tab for fans. Images and videos are capped at 10 across the whole description. Replaces the retired Info sections (and the D335 “media between paragraphs” layout).

活動的「描述」，由區塊組成（D354，2026-10-05；規格 5.1.6.1 §4.2 F2、5.1.6.2 F2、主規格 §7.10）。區塊三種——文字、圖片、影片；每個區塊都沒有標題、都可以拖動排序、都可以刪除，粉絲照這個順序讀。第一個文字區塊就是必填的描述：至少一個文字區塊有字才算填了（只有圖片影片不算），所以最後一個文字區塊刪不掉。「新增描述」加一個文字區塊。從文字區塊插入的圖片或影片自成一個區塊，排在那個文字區塊之後。文字區塊的格式：粗體、斜體、連結、編號清單、項目符號清單、分隔線；連結只收 http／https，粉絲端在新分頁開啟。圖片與影片整份描述合計最多 10 個。取代退場的 Info sections（與 D335「媒體夾在段落之間」的版面）。

Relationships

元件關係

Built from由哪些元件組成 ↓
moleculeCard (.card--muted)
atomInput (.textarea look, link URL) Upload tile Button Icon
Each block is a .card--muted (L2, Q66) laid out handle | content | remove — the layout that came over from the retired Info sections. A text block is a contenteditable box wearing the .textarea look; a media block is an Upload tile with no slot ratio (the frame follows the media’s own shape) and its own remove button hidden, since the block’s ✕ removes it. The format buttons’ pressed state borrows the nav “selected” tint (--selected-surface); links are underlined in the text colour, not brand orange.每個區塊是一張 .card--muted（L2，Q66），版面＝把手｜內容｜刪除——從退場的 Info sections 搬來。文字區塊是穿 .textarea 外觀的 contenteditable；媒體區塊就是 Upload tile，不帶槽位比例（框照媒體本身的形狀），自己的刪除鈕收起，刪除走區塊的 ✕。格式鈕按下態借導覽類「已選」的淡橘（--selected-surface）；連結用字色加底線，不用品牌橘。

In a two-step edit panel (event detail) the view state is a readout: blocks lose their card, the toolbar, link row and “Add description” are hidden, handles and remove buttons hide with the other buttons, text shows its formatting without a box, and media stay visible without the replace button.

在兩段式編輯面板（活動詳情）裡，檢視態就是讀數：區塊拿掉卡面，工具列、連結列與「新增描述」收起，把手與刪除鈕跟其他按鈕一起收起，文字照格式顯示、沒有框，媒體照常顯示、沒有替換鈕。

For developers — classes · code給工程師 — class · 程式碼

.rich-body（flex column、gap: --sp-12）> .rich-body__blocks（gap: --sp-12）> .rich-body__block.card.card--muted[data-rb-block="text|image|video"] × N（--card-pad: --sp-12，flex 橫排：.rich-body__grip ＋ .rich-body__main〔文字：.rich-body__tools（.rich-body__fmt 六顆 .btn--ghost.btn--sm.btn--icon、aria-pressed；.rich-body__ins 直線＋兩顆插入鈕）＋ .rich-body__linkbar（.input＋套用／移除連結／取消＋.field__error）＋ .textarea.rich-body__text[contenteditable]；媒體：.upload-tile.rich-body__media〕＋ .rich-body__remove）＋ .rich-body__foot（.rich-body__add＋.field__hint.rich-body__limit）。狀態 .rich-body--has-media、.rich-body__block.is-dragging、.rich-body__block.is-locked。內文只含 p／br／strong／em／a／ul／ol／li／hr。

互動 partials/rich-body.js：window.ztorRichBody.mount(host, { blocks|text, rows, placeholder, placeholderKey, label, labelKey, proxy, maxMedia:10, feat, fmtFeat, addFeat, onChange }) → { el, get(), text(), media(), set(v), lock(on, opts?), add(), insert(type, fileOrSrc, row?), format(cmd, row?), move(from, to) }（同一物件掛在 host.ztorRichBody）。blocks＝[{ type:'text', text, html }, { type:'image'|'video', src }]，每個文字區塊各一筆、不合併。粗體／斜體走 execCommand；清單與分隔線自己動 DOM（Chrome 的 insertOrderedList 會產生 <p><ol>）；連結輸入列只收 http／https（safeUrl()），讀回一律經 sanitize() 補 target=_blank rel=noopener noreferrer。排序：按住把手才 draggable、把手聚焦上／下鍵移一格。lock(true) 鎖第一個文字區塊＋緊接的媒體（只鎖第 1 段，仍可拖動）；lock(true, { all: true }) 整份鎖定（D362，bookyay 帶入的活動，兩個消費頁都用這個）：每個區塊 .is-locked、host 掛 .rich-body--locked 收起把手與底部，add()／insert()／move() 不動作，set() 重畫後維持鎖定。靜態工具：toBlocks／normalize／textOf／mediaOf／hasMedia／hasText／sanitize／plainOf／textToHtml／isFormatted／safeUrl／fromHtml／migrate／fieldKeys／textFields；翻譯欄位＝每個有字的文字區塊一格（只有一格時沿用 base key），Fan event page 用同一支對回去。

ds-components/rich-body.css ＋ partials/rich-body.js（媒體塊靠 partials/upload-tile.js 增強；圖示 bold／italic／link／list-numbers／list／separator／grip-vertical 在 js/icons.js）。消費頁：create-event.html（步驟 2 描述，data-feat：新增描述 S67、插入 S58、格式 S59）、event-detail.html（活動內容的描述）；只用靜態工具的：event-localization.html、js/fan-event-page.js。

4.223 Follow field

🟡 molecule

Purpose

用途

A ticket bundle’s four schedule times — listing, unlist, sale start, sale end — follow the event by default and can each be set on their own (D342, 2026-10-01; main spec §7.14 “Event sales time layers” items 5–7). The four times come in two pairs — listing + unlisting, sale start + sale end — each pair its own framed row (same look as “Show in shop”) with one “Follow event” switch: on follows the event for both, off sets both on their own. Following is live: change the event and the readout changes. A custom time must fall within the event’s listing period and can open sales before the event does (a pre-sale).

票務商品的四個排程時間——上架、下架、開賣、停售——預設跟隨活動、可以逐格另設（D342，2026-10-01；主規格 §7.14「活動販售的時間層級」第 5–7 條）。四個時間兩兩一組（上架＋下架、開賣＋停售），每組一條「顯示於商店」同款的外框列，右邊一顆「跟隨活動」開關（開＝兩格都跟隨、關＝兩格都另設）。跟隨是即時連動：活動改了，讀數跟著變。另設的時間須落在活動上架區間內，可以早於活動開賣（組合包預售）。

Relationships

元件關係

Built from由哪些元件組成 ↓
atomSwitch Input Field system
Only for bundles with event tickets. It replaces the schedule fields inside Listing controls; the listing and show switches stay.只用在含活動票券的組合包。它取代 Listing controls 裡的排程欄位；上架與顯示開關照舊。

Rendered preview — one pair following, one pair custom (one out of range)

視覺展示 — 一組跟隨、一組另設（其中一格超出上架區間）

Outside the event’s listing period (2026/09/20 12:00 – no end).超出活動上架區間（2026/09/20 12:00 – 不自動下架）。

Following tracks the event — change the event and these change too. A custom time must fall within the event’s listing period and can open sales before the event does.跟隨＝活動改了跟著改。另設的時間須在活動上架區間內，可以早於活動開賣。

For developers — classes · code給工程師 — class · 程式碼

.follow-fields（grid、gap: --sp-16）> .follow-field[data-tb-sched][data-state] × N（一條外框列，--radius-xl＋--layer-line 內描邊）> .follow-field__head（左 .follow-field__txt＝__t 標題＋__s 讀數；右 .follow-field__follow＝「跟隨活動」小字＋ .switch[role=switch]）＋ .input[type=datetime-local][data-tb-at]（另設時）＋ .field__error[data-tb-err]。

markup 與互動一律由 js/ticket-bundle.js（window.ZtorTicketBundle）提供：schedHTML(st, et, T, { keys, note, disabled })、onClick(e, st, ctx)、onInput(e, st)、syncErrors(root, st, ctx, T)、effective(st, et)。D363（2026-10-07）：times(ev) 另帶活動的 shown／unlisted，evHidden(et) 判斷活動隱藏中（消費頁的組合包顯示開關停用、畫成關）；停售另設與購買條件限時間的結束端多一道上限 lateEnd(v, et)（不能晚於活動停售，開始端可早於開賣）。消費頁：js/bundle-editor.js（建立活動第 5 步）、bundle-detail.html、create-bundle.html。同一支 JS 另產「購買條件與限購」的讀數與編輯區，重用 .rule／.rule-sub／.rule-row（Ticket tier card）、Radio list、KV list、Control row，不另立 CSS。

4.224 Size mix

🟡 molecule

Purpose

用途

How one market’s (or one product’s) demand splits across sizes (D360, demand board 5.1.5.16 F4.1). Sizes are ordered, so it’s a row of small bars in size order — you see at a glance whether a market leans small or large. Every size gets a fixed-width slot, so the same size sits in the same column on every row and rows compare straight down. Bars are scaled to the row’s largest size (shares usually run 5–45%; scaled to 100% they’d all be flat); the number under the bar carries the exact value. The largest size gets the brand colour. The number can be the share or the suggested quantity — the bar shape stays the share either way.

一個市場（或一件商品）的需求在各尺寸之間怎麼分（D360，需求看板 5.1.5.16 F4.1）。尺寸有順序，所以畫成依序排列的一排小直條，一眼看出偏小碼還是偏大碼。每個尺寸固定一格，整張表的同一尺寸落在同一欄、上下列直接比。條高以這一列最大的尺寸為滿格（占比通常 5%–45%，以 100% 為滿格條都會很矮），精確值寫在條下；最大那格上品牌色。數字可以是占比或建議生產量，條形永遠是占比。

Relationships

元件關係

Used by被哪些元件使用 ↑
organismDemand table
Why not Split bar: segment widths move with the data, so the same size lands in a different place on every row and rows can’t be compared.為什麼不用 Split bar：分段寬度隨資料變，同一尺寸在每列的位置都不同，上下列沒辦法對著比。

Rendered preview — header, share row, suggested-quantity row, a size with no demand, standalone (labeled)

視覺展示 — 表頭、占比列、建議生產量列、某尺寸沒有需求、單獨使用（帶尺寸名）

XSSMLXL —48%38%12%3% —7257184
12%S30%M40%L18%XL
For developers — classes · code給工程師 — class · 程式碼

.size-mix（grid，欄數＝ inline --size-mix-n，格寬 --size-mix-slot＝--sp-40）> .size-mix__slot（--peak／--none）> .size-mix__bar > .size-mix__fill（inline --share 0–1，相對該列最大值）＋ .size-mix__val（＋ --labeled 時 .size-mix__label）。表頭 .size-mix--head 只放 .size-mix__label。

Markup 一律由 window.ztorDemandView.sizeMixHTML(sizes, sizeRows, { mode:'share'|'qty', labeled }) 與 sizeMixHeadHTML(sizes) 產生（js/demand-board.js），資料取 window.ztorDemand 結果的 sizes／sizeRows。

4.225 Demand table

🟠 organism

Purpose

用途

The market demand table on the demand board (D360, 5.1.5.16 F4): one row per country, expandable to cities; demand index, size mix, suggested quantity, planned quantity (typed in), gap. Not a new table — rows are the list-page Product list rows and city expansion is Row disclosure (Q107/Q109); this file only adds the --demand column track and the cells unique to this table. Click a row to make it the focus (brand tint, Q8); Unspecified region always sits last, shows “—” for the index and can’t be focused. A gap where the suggestion beats the plan is called out with a warning badge. Status lines reuse Badge: info for “Similar products: T-Shirt”, neutral for “Not enough data”.

需求看板的市場需求表（D360，5.1.5.16 F4）：每個國家一列、可展開城市；需求指數、各尺寸、建議生產量、計畫生產量（可填）、差距。不是新的表格元件——列沿用清單頁的 Product list，展開城市沿用 Row disclosure（Q107／Q109），本檔只加 --demand 欄軌與這張表特有的格。點列＝選定對象（品牌色淡底，Q8）；未提供地區永遠排最後、指數寫「—」、不可選定。建議多於計畫的差距用 warning 徽章加強提示。狀態行沿用 Badge：info＝「參考同類商品：T-Shirt」、neutral＝「資料不足」。

4.226 Demand focus

🟠 organism

Purpose

用途

The focus panel beside the market table (D360, 5.1.5.16 F3/F4.1/F4.2). Click a market and this card turns into that market: days to sell out and size gap (the two summary metrics that only make sense for one focus), then a per-size table where you fill “market + size” planned quantities and pick a size to narrow the focus. It lives beside the table, not in the summary row, so clicking a row never pushes the row you’re looking at down the page. When a metric can’t be computed yet the value reads “—” and the line under it says what’s missing. Below 1440px the row stacks and the panel sits under the table.

市場需求表旁的選定對象面板（D360，5.1.5.16 F3／F4.1／F4.2）。點一個市場，這張卡就換成那個市場：先是預測售罄天數與尺寸差距（摘要指標裡只對選定對象有意義的兩項），再來是各尺寸表——在這裡填「市場＋尺寸層」的計畫生產量、點一個尺寸把選定對象收窄到那個尺寸。放在表格旁而不是頁首摘要列：點列時不會把正在看的那一列往下推。指標還算不出來時數值寫「—」、底下一行寫缺什麼。視窗 < 1440px 時這一排改單欄，面板落到表格下方。

For developers — classes · code給工程師 — class · 程式碼

.card.demand-focus（flex column，gap --sp-16，Q103）> .demand-focus__head（__titles > __kicker ＋ __title；右側 .btn--ghost.btn--sm 取消選定）＋ .demand-focus__empty 或 .stat-row.demand-focus__stats ＋ .demand-focus__note（徽章）＋ .variant-table-wrap > .variant-table.variant-table--demand-sizes（五欄可壓縮、min-width:0；選定尺寸 .demand-size--focus）。

由 window.ztorDemandView.create(root, cfg) 的 [data-dv="focus"] 插槽產生（js/demand-board.js）；view.state.focus／focusSize 是目前的選定對象。消費頁：demand-board.html（F4 右側常駐欄，.bento__aside）。

4.227 Condition list

🟡 molecule

Purpose

用途

A list of different conditions, each added at most once. It starts empty; “Add condition” opens a menu of the kinds not added yet, and every card can be removed. Built for event purchase rules (D366/D367): the event-level defaults and a single ticket’s purchase conditions share it. A card imported from bookyay is locked — fields disabled, no remove button, source tag in its top row. Since D388 the event detail page uses it too, for editing a self-built event’s rules after publishing; both pages draw it from the shared script js/purchase-conds.js. Since D389 the same script also checks timed conditions: a window outside the event’s listing period shows an error under that card’s dates and blocks saving, on both pages. Since D417 a locked set can still offer a few kinds (ctx.addOnly): on a bookyay ticket the imported limits stay locked, and in the final version the add menu lists only Discount and Limited-time discount (tagged S96); cards the creator adds that way can be edited and removed. A bookyay limits card shows only the per-person and per-order fields.

「好幾種不同的條件，各加一次」的清單。一開始是空的，「新增條件」選單只列還沒加的種類，每張卡都能移除。為活動購票規則而建（D366／D367）：用在單張門票的購買條件（2026-10-20 D460 起活動層預設已永久移除，不再有活動層那一份）。從 bookyay 帶入的卡是鎖定的——欄位停用、沒有移除鈕、頂列掛來源標記。D388 起活動詳情也用它（自建活動發布後改購買條件），兩頁都由共用腳本 js/purchase-conds.js 畫。D389 起同一支腳本也做限時條件的期間檢查：期間超出活動上架區間時，那張卡的日期欄下出現紅字、擋下儲存，兩頁一致。D417 起整組鎖定時也能只開放少數種類（ctx.addOnly）：bookyay 門票帶入的限購照舊鎖定，最終版的新增選單只列「折扣」「限時折扣」（掛 S96），這樣加進來的卡可以改、可以移除。bookyay 的限購卡只畫每人總限購與每次交易限購兩格。

2026-10-15: a condition imported from bookyay always carries the source tag on its card header — also when the whole set is locked (replaces the old “no repeat on a fully locked set” rule); the ticket dialogs’ “Purchase rules” section titles no longer carry their own tag. Locked inputs use the sitewide .input:disabled (Q133).

2026-10-15：bookyay 帶入的條件卡一律在卡頂列掛來源標記——整組鎖定時也掛（取代原本「整組鎖定時卡上不重複」的做法）；單張門票彈窗「購買條件」區塊標題不再另掛一枚。停用欄位照全站 .input:disabled（Q133）。

2026-10-16 (D428): a purchase-limits card left empty shows an inline error under its field (“Enter a limit.”) as soon as it’s added, and saving or publishing is blocked until it’s filled. Cards brought in from bookyay already carry their value and aren’t checked.

2026-10-16（D428）：「限購」卡的每人總限購數量空白時，加入後就在那一格下方就地紅字「請填上限。」，填好才能儲存或發布；bookyay 帶入鎖定的卡值已帶入、不檢查。

4.228 Venue map

🟡 molecule

Purpose

用途

The event’s location map, made from its full address (D369 decision 2, 2026-10-07). There is no map field and the pin can’t be dragged — the same address always gives the same place, whether the event was imported from bookyay or created here. This prototype is self-contained and doesn’t call a map service, so the map is a static stand-in: a drawn street tile (one of four layouts picked from the address text), a centre pin, the address, and an “Open in Maps” link that searches the address text (a plain link — nothing is embedded). When the real map lands, only the canvas content changes.

活動的地點地圖，依完整地址產生（D369 決定二，2026-10-07）。地圖不另設欄位、定位針不能拖——同一個地址就是同一個位置，bookyay 帶入或自建都一樣。原型自給自足、不接地圖服務，所以畫面是靜態示意：自繪街道底圖（依地址文字挑四種擺法之一）、中央定位針、地址一行，以及用地址文字搜尋的「在地圖中開啟」連結（只是一般連結，不嵌入任何外部內容）。正式接上真地圖時只換畫布內容。

Relationships

元件關係

Built from由哪些元件組成 ↓

Colours go through the component’s own --vm-* role variables: site tokens by default, the front-end tokens when it sits inside the fan event page mirror (.fep-shop) — the mirror file itself gets no new rules.顏色走元件自己的 --vm-* 角色變數：預設接站上 token，放進粉絲活動頁鏡像（.fep-shop）時改接前台 token——鏡像檔本身不加規則。

Rendered preview — fan event page block (16:9) and the form preview strip (3:1, link only)

4.229 Vault item task

🟡 molecule

Purpose

用途

The Media Vault’s second layer (D377, 2026-10-08). Getting into a vault is the first layer — ways in plus keys, shown by the Media vault door. Inside, each item can carry one task of its own: no task, an action task (nine kinds) or a tier task. Naming rule: “key” only ever means the first-layer pass; the second layer is always a “task”. This component owns two things: the small tags hung on every item (what the task is, how many fans can see it, grand surprise, and — when viewing as a tier — that tier’s result), and the task section inside the item drawer.

媒體庫的第二層（D377，2026-10-08）。進得了庫是第一層——進庫方法＋鑰匙，由 Media vault 的門條負責；進庫之後，每一件內容可以各自設一個任務：不設、行動任務（九種）或等級任務。命名規則：「鑰匙」只指第一層的進庫憑證，第二層一律叫「任務」。本元件管兩件事：掛在每一件內容上的小標（任務是什麼、幾人看得到、大彩蛋、以分級檢視時該分級的結果），以及單件抽屜裡的任務區。
